"""Follow-up pelanggan, servis berkala, garansi, dan piutang (Tahap 13).

Pengingat disusun dari data nota (sumber tunggal), lalu disalin ke tabel
`followup_items` agar status, penanggung jawab, dan riwayat perpindahan tidak
hilang. Tidak ada pesan otomatis: tombol follow-up hanya membuka WhatsApp dan
mencatat upaya.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import calendar
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload, selectinload

from database.models import (
    FollowupEvent,
    FollowupItem,
    ServiceRecord,
    VehicleOrUnit,
    WhatsAppLog,
)
from services import payment_service, settings_service, whatsapp_service
from services.auth_service import Operator
from services.customer_service import describe_unit
from services.errors import BusinessValidationError, ConflictError, NotFoundError
from services.utils import is_valid_whatsapp, mask_phone, money

JENIS_PROSPEK = "prospek"
JENIS_SERVIS = "servis_berkala"
JENIS_GARANSI = "garansi"
JENIS_PIUTANG = "piutang"
JENIS_VALUES = {JENIS_PROSPEK, JENIS_SERVIS, JENIS_GARANSI, JENIS_PIUTANG}
JENIS_LABELS = {
    JENIS_PROSPEK: "Prospek",
    JENIS_SERVIS: "Servis berkala",
    JENIS_GARANSI: "Garansi",
    JENIS_PIUTANG: "Piutang",
}

STATUS_BARU = "BARU"
STATUS_SUDAH = "SUDAH_FOLLOWUP"
STATUS_JANJI = "JANJI_BAYAR"
STATUS_KENDALA = "MASIH_KENDALA"
STATUS_SELESAI = "SELESAI"
STATUS_ARSIP = "ARSIP"
STATUS_AKAN_DATANG = "AKAN_DATANG"
STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG = "TIDAK_DATANG"
STATUS_VALUES = {
    STATUS_BARU,
    STATUS_SUDAH,
    STATUS_JANJI,
    STATUS_KENDALA,
    STATUS_SELESAI,
    STATUS_ARSIP,
    STATUS_AKAN_DATANG,
    STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG,
}
STATUS_LABELS = {
    STATUS_BARU: "Baru",
    STATUS_SUDAH: "Sudah dihubungi",
    STATUS_JANJI: "Janji bayar",
    STATUS_KENDALA: "Masih kendala",
    STATUS_SELESAI: "Selesai",
    STATUS_ARSIP: "Arsip",
    STATUS_AKAN_DATANG: "Akan datang service",
    STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG: "Tidak akan datang service",
}
OPEN_STATUSES = {
    STATUS_BARU,
    STATUS_SUDAH,
    STATUS_JANJI,
    STATUS_KENDALA,
    STATUS_AKAN_DATANG,
}
JADWAL_JENIS = {JENIS_SERVIS, JENIS_GARANSI}

SYSTEM_OPERATOR = "Sistem"
WA_JENIS = {
    JENIS_PROSPEK: whatsapp_service.JENIS_FOLLOWUP,
    JENIS_SERVIS: whatsapp_service.JENIS_FOLLOWUP,
    JENIS_GARANSI: whatsapp_service.JENIS_GARANSI,
    JENIS_PIUTANG: whatsapp_service.JENIS_PIUTANG,
}


def add_months(day: date, months: int) -> date:
    """Tambah bulan dengan kalender; tanggal 31 mengikuti akhir bulan tujuan."""
    months = int(months)
    if months == 0:
        return day
    total = day.month - 1 + months
    year = day.year + total // 12
    month = total % 12 + 1
    last_day = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1]
    return date(year, month, min(day.day, last_day))


def _format_date(day: Optional[date]) -> str:
    return day.strftime("%d/%m/%Y") if day else "-"


def _label_acuan(record: ServiceRecord) -> str:
    return ", ".join(job.deskripsi for job in record.jobs) or "servis"


def _sisa(record: ServiceRecord) -> Decimal:
    return payment_service.record_sisa(record)


def _fully_returned(record: ServiceRecord) -> bool:
    return money(
        Decimal(record.total_servis or 0) - Decimal(record.total_retur or 0)
    ) <= 0


def _load_records(db: Session) -> List[ServiceRecord]:
    statement = (
        select(ServiceRecord)
        .where(ServiceRecord.status == "NORMAL")
        .options(
            joinedload(ServiceRecord.unit).joinedload(VehicleOrUnit.customer),
            selectinload(ServiceRecord.jobs),
            selectinload(ServiceRecord.parts),
        )
        .order_by(ServiceRecord.waktu_service.desc(), ServiceRecord.id.desc())
    )
    return list(db.scalars(statement).unique().all())


def _record_source(
    jenis: str,
    record: ServiceRecord,
    jatuh_tempo: date,
    judul: str,
    rincian: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "jenis": jenis,
        "customer": record.unit.customer,
        "unit": record.unit,
        "record": record,
        "jatuh_tempo": jatuh_tempo,
        "judul": judul,
        "rincian": rincian,
    }


def _collect_sources(
    db: Session, today: date, ops: Dict[str, Any]
) -> Dict[Tuple[str, int], Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Hitung pengingat aktif dari nota; satu sumber per pelanggan/jenis."""
    records = _load_records(db)
    by_customer: Dict[int, List[ServiceRecord]] = {}
    for record in records:
        by_customer.setdefault(record.unit.customer_id, []).append(record)

    horizon_garansi = today + timedelta(days=int(ops["garansi_akan_habis_hari"]))
    horizon_servis = today + timedelta(days=int(ops["servis_berkala_awal_hari"]))
    prospek_hari = int(ops["prospek_hari"])

    sources: Dict[Tuple[str, int], Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    for customer_id, customer_records in by_customer.items():
        latest = customer_records[0]

        # --- Prospek pasca servis ---
        latest_day = latest.waktu_service.date()
        age = (today - latest_day).days
        if age >= prospek_hari and is_valid_whatsapp(latest.unit.customer.nomor_hp):
            sources[(JENIS_PROSPEK, customer_id)] = _record_source(
                JENIS_PROSPEK,
                latest,
                latest_day + timedelta(days=prospek_hari),
                f"Follow up pasca servis {describe_unit(latest.unit)}",
                [
                    {
                        "no_nota": latest.no_nota,
                        "tanggal_servis": latest_day,
                        "umur_hari": age,
                        "pekerjaan": _label_acuan(latest),
                        "garansi_sampai": latest.garansi_sampai
                        if latest.bergaransi
                        else None,
                    }
                ],
            )

        # --- Garansi akan habis ---
        garansi_record: Optional[ServiceRecord] = None
        for record in customer_records:
            if _fully_returned(record):
                continue
            if not record.bergaransi or record.garansi_sampai is None:
                continue
            if record.status_klaim == "Sudah claim":
                continue
            if today <= record.garansi_sampai <= horizon_garansi:
                if (
                    garansi_record is None
                    or record.garansi_sampai < garansi_record.garansi_sampai
                ):
                    garansi_record = record
        if garansi_record is not None:
            sources[(JENIS_GARANSI, customer_id)] = _record_source(
                JENIS_GARANSI,
                garansi_record,
                garansi_record.garansi_sampai,
                f"Garansi {describe_unit(garansi_record.unit)} berakhir "
                f"{_format_date(garansi_record.garansi_sampai)}",
                [
                    {
                        "no_nota": garansi_record.no_nota,
                        "tanggal_servis": garansi_record.waktu_service.date(),
                        "pekerjaan": _label_acuan(garansi_record),
                        "garansi_sampai": garansi_record.garansi_sampai,
                        "sisa_hari": (garansi_record.garansi_sampai - today).days,
                    }
                ],
            )

        # --- Servis berkala dari baris jasa dan sparepart nota ---
        seen_rows: set = set()
        servis_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for record in customer_records:
            if _fully_returned(record):
                continue
            for job in record.jobs:
                interval = int(getattr(job, "interval_servis_bulan", 0) or 0)
                if interval <= 0:
                    continue
                key = (
                    "jasa",
                    job.harga_jasa_id
                    if job.harga_jasa_id is not None
                    else (job.deskripsi or "").strip().lower(),
                )
                if key in seen_rows:
                    continue
                seen_rows.add(key)
                servis_day = record.waktu_service.date()
                servis_rows.append(
                    {
                        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
                        "jenis_item": "jasa",
                        "nama": job.deskripsi,
                        "pekerjaan": job.deskripsi,
                        "tanggal_servis": servis_day,
                        "interval_bulan": interval,
                        "jatuh_tempo": add_months(servis_day, interval),
                        "record": record,
                    }
                )
            for part in record.parts:
                interval = int(getattr(part, "interval_servis_bulan", 0) or 0)
                if interval <= 0:
                    continue
                key = (
                    "part",
                    part.stock_item_id
                    if part.stock_item_id is not None
                    else (part.nama_part or "").strip().lower(),
                )
                if key in seen_rows:
                    continue
                seen_rows.add(key)
                servis_day = record.waktu_service.date()
                label = f"Sparepart {part.nama_part}"
                servis_rows.append(
                    {
                        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
                        "jenis_item": "sparepart",
                        "nama": part.nama_part,
                        "pekerjaan": label,
                        "tanggal_servis": servis_day,
                        "interval_bulan": interval,
                        "jatuh_tempo": add_months(servis_day, interval),
                        "record": record,
                    }
                )
        due_rows = [row for row in servis_rows if row["jatuh_tempo"] <= horizon_servis]
        if due_rows:
            nearest = min(
                due_rows,
                key=lambda row: (row["jatuh_tempo"], row["no_nota"]),
            )
            record = nearest["record"]
            sources[(JENIS_SERVIS, customer_id)] = _record_source(
                JENIS_SERVIS,
                record,
                nearest["jatuh_tempo"],
                f"Servis berkala {describe_unit(record.unit)}: "
                f"{nearest['pekerjaan']}",
                [
                    {
                        "no_nota": row["no_nota"],
                        "jenis_item": row["jenis_item"],
                        "nama": row["nama"],
                        "pekerjaan": row["pekerjaan"],
                        "tanggal_servis": row["tanggal_servis"],
                        "interval_bulan": row["interval_bulan"],
                        "jatuh_tempo": row["jatuh_tempo"],
                    }
                    for row in sorted(
                        servis_rows, key=lambda row: row["jatuh_tempo"]
                    )
                    if row["record"].id == record.id
                ],
            )

        # --- Piutang (digabung per pelanggan) ---
        unpaid = [
            record
            for record in customer_records
            if record.status_bayar == "BELUM_LUNAS" and _sisa(record) > 0
        ]
        if unpaid:
            nearest_due = min(
                (
                    record.jatuh_tempo
                    for record in unpaid
                    if record.jatuh_tempo is not None
                ),
                default=None,
            )
            anchor = next(
                (record for record in unpaid if record.jatuh_tempo == nearest_due),
                unpaid[0],
            )
            total_sisa = sum((_sisa(record) for record in unpaid), Decimal("0.00"))
            sources[(JENIS_PIUTANG, customer_id)] = _record_source(
                JENIS_PIUTANG,
                anchor,
                nearest_due or anchor.waktu_service.date(),
                f"Piutang {len(unpaid)} nota",
                [
                    {
                        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
                        "tanggal_servis": record.waktu_service.date(),
                        "total_servis": record.total_servis,
                        "sisa_bayar": _sisa(record),
                        "jatuh_tempo": record.jatuh_tempo,
                    }
                    for record in sorted(
                        unpaid, key=lambda row: row.waktu_service
                    )
                ],
            )

    return sources


def _same_cycle(item: FollowupItem, source: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    record = source["record"]
    return (
        item.service_record_id == record.id
        and item.jatuh_tempo == source["jatuh_tempo"]
    )


def _add_event(
    db: Session,
    item: FollowupItem,
    from_status: Optional[str],
    to_status: str,
    reason: Optional[str],
    operator: str,
    now: datetime,
) -> FollowupEvent:
    event = FollowupEvent(
        item_id=item.id,
        from_status=from_status,
        to_status=to_status,
        alasan=reason,
        operator=operator,
        created_at=now,
    )
    db.add(event)
    return event


def _update_item(
    db: Session,
    item: FollowupItem,
    source: Dict[str, Any],
    now: datetime,
) -> bool:
    """Perbarui item dari sumber; True bila siklus baru memulai status BARU."""
    record = source["record"]
    new_cycle = (
        item.service_record_id is not None
        and item.service_record_id != record.id
        and item.jenis in {JENIS_PROSPEK, JENIS_SERVIS, JENIS_GARANSI}
    )
    item.unit_id = record.unit_id
    item.service_record_id = record.id
    item.judul = source["judul"]
    item.jatuh_tempo = source["jatuh_tempo"]
    if new_cycle:
        previous = item.status
        item.status = STATUS_BARU
        item.followup_terakhir = None
        item.janji_bayar = None
        item.tanggal_service = None
        item.alasan_arsip = None
        item.archived_at = None
        _add_event(
            db,
            item,
            previous,
            STATUS_BARU,
            "Siklus baru dari nota terbaru.",
            SYSTEM_OPERATOR,
            now,
        )
    return new_cycle


def _archive_item(
    db: Session,
    item: FollowupItem,
    reason: str,
    operator: str,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    previous = item.status
    item.status = STATUS_ARSIP
    item.alasan_arsip = reason
    item.archived_at = now
    _add_event(db, item, previous, STATUS_ARSIP, reason, operator, now)


def sync_items(
    db: Session,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
    commit: bool = True,
) -> Dict[Tuple[str, int], Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Sinkronkan pengingat aktif dari nota tanpa menghapus riwayat."""
    day = today or date.today()
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
    sources = _collect_sources(db, day, ops)
    items = list(db.scalars(select(FollowupItem)).all())

    by_key: Dict[Tuple[str, int], List[FollowupItem]] = {}
    for item in items:
        by_key.setdefault((item.jenis, item.customer_id), []).append(item)

    for key, source in sources.items():
        rows = by_key.get(key, [])
        active = next((row for row in rows if row.status != STATUS_ARSIP), None)
        if active is not None:
            _update_item(db, active, source, moment)
            continue
        if any(row.status == STATUS_ARSIP and _same_cycle(row, source) for row in rows):
            # Pengingat untuk siklus ini sudah diarsipkan manual.
            continue
        item = FollowupItem(
            jenis=key[0],
            customer_id=key[1],
            unit_id=source["record"].unit_id,
            service_record_id=source["record"].id,
            judul=source["judul"],
            jatuh_tempo=source["jatuh_tempo"],
            status=STATUS_BARU,
            created_at=moment,
            updated_at=moment,
        )
        db.add(item)
        db.flush()
        _add_event(db, item, None, STATUS_BARU, "Pengingat baru.", SYSTEM_OPERATOR, moment)

    for key, rows in by_key.items():
        if key in sources:
            continue
        for item in rows:
            if item.status == STATUS_ARSIP:
                continue
            _archive_item(
                db,
                item,
                "Sumber pengingat sudah tidak aktif.",
                SYSTEM_OPERATOR,
                moment,
            )

    db.flush()
    if commit:
        db.commit()
    return sources


def _serialize(
    item: FollowupItem,
    source: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    ops: Dict[str, Any],
    today: date,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    customer = item.customer
    unit = item.unit
    record = item.service_record
    sisa_hari = (item.jatuh_tempo - today).days if item.jatuh_tempo else None
    cooldown = int(ops.get("cooldown_hari", 7))
    cooldown_until = (
        item.followup_terakhir + timedelta(days=cooldown)
        if item.followup_terakhir is not None and cooldown > 0
        else None
    )
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    valid_number = is_valid_whatsapp(customer.nomor_hp)
    on_cooldown = cooldown_until is not None and moment < cooldown_until
    bisa = (
        item.status not in {STATUS_ARSIP, STATUS_SELESAI, STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG}
        and valid_number
        and not on_cooldown
        and settings_service.within_followup_window(ops, moment)
    )
    status_label = STATUS_LABELS.get(item.status, item.status)
    if item.jenis == JENIS_PROSPEK and item.status == STATUS_BARU:
        status_label = "Masih bagus"
    return {
        "id": item.id,
        "jenis": item.jenis,
        "jenis_label": JENIS_LABELS.get(item.jenis, item.jenis),
        "customer_id": item.customer_id,
        "customer_nama": customer.nama,
        "customer_alamat": customer.alamat,
        "nomor_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
        "nomor_masked": mask_phone(customer.nomor_hp),
        "unit_id": item.unit_id,
        "unit_deskripsi": describe_unit(unit) if unit is not None else "-",
        "service_record_id": item.service_record_id,
        "no_nota": record.no_nota if record is not None else None,
        "judul": item.judul,
        "jatuh_tempo": item.jatuh_tempo,
        "sisa_hari": sisa_hari,
        "terlambat": bool(
            item.status != STATUS_AKAN_DATANG
            and sisa_hari is not None
            and sisa_hari < 0
        ),
        "status": item.status,
        "status_label": status_label,
        "followup_terakhir": item.followup_terakhir,
        "sudah_followup": item.followup_terakhir is not None,
        "janji_bayar": item.janji_bayar,
        "tanggal_service": item.tanggal_service,
        "alasan_arsip": item.alasan_arsip,
        "cooldown_sampai": cooldown_until,
        "bisa_followup": bisa,
        "rincian": list(source["rincian"]) if source else [],
    }


def _item_statement(jenis: Optional[str], customer_id: Optional[int]):
    statement = select(FollowupItem)
    if jenis:
        statement = statement.where(FollowupItem.jenis == jenis)
    if customer_id is not None:
        statement = statement.where(FollowupItem.customer_id == customer_id)
    return statement.options(
        joinedload(FollowupItem.customer),
        joinedload(FollowupItem.unit),
        joinedload(FollowupItem.service_record),
    )


def _active_items(
    db: Session,
    jenis: Optional[str] = None,
    customer_id: Optional[int] = None,
) -> List[FollowupItem]:
    statement = _item_statement(jenis, customer_id).where(
        FollowupItem.status.notin_(
            [STATUS_ARSIP, STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG, STATUS_SELESAI]
        )
    )
    return list(db.scalars(statement).unique().all())


def _archived_items(
    db: Session,
    jenis: Optional[str] = None,
    customer_id: Optional[int] = None,
) -> List[FollowupItem]:
    statement = _item_statement(jenis, customer_id).where(
        FollowupItem.status == STATUS_ARSIP
    )
    return list(db.scalars(statement).unique().all())


def list_items(
    db: Session,
    filter: str = "semua",
    jenis: Optional[str] = None,
    customer_id: Optional[int] = None,
    status: Optional[str] = None,
    exclude_status: Optional[str] = None,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
    sync: bool = True,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    day = today or date.today()
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    sources = sync_items(db, today=day, now=moment) if sync else {}
    ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)

    if filter == "arsip":
        items = _archived_items(db, jenis, customer_id)
        items.sort(
            key=lambda item: (
                item.archived_at or datetime.min,
                item.id,
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        serialized = [
            _serialize(item, sources.get((item.jenis, item.customer_id)), ops, day, moment)
            for item in items
        ]
        return {"items": serialized, "jumlah": len(serialized)}

    items = _active_items(db, jenis, customer_id)
    if status:
        items = [item for item in items if item.status == status]
    if exclude_status:
        items = [item for item in items if item.status != exclude_status]
    if filter == "hari_ini":
        items = [
            item
            for item in items
            if item.jatuh_tempo is not None
            and item.jatuh_tempo <= day
            and item.status in OPEN_STATUSES
        ]
    elif filter == "terlambat":
        items = [
            item
            for item in items
            if item.jatuh_tempo is not None
            and item.jatuh_tempo < day
            and item.status in OPEN_STATUSES
        ]
    elif filter not in {"semua", ""}:
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Filter tugas harus semua, hari_ini, terlambat, atau arsip."
        )

    items.sort(
        key=lambda item: (
            item.jatuh_tempo or date.max,
            item.customer.nama.lower(),
            item.id,
        )
    )
    serialized = []
    for item in items:
        source = sources.get((item.jenis, item.customer_id))
        serialized.append(_serialize(item, source, ops, day, moment))
    return {"items": serialized, "jumlah": len(serialized)}


def counts(
    db: Session,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """Angka kartu Ringkasan; dihitung dari item aktif yang sama."""
    day = today or date.today()
    sync_items(db, today=day, now=now)
    rows = db.execute(
        select(FollowupItem.jenis, FollowupItem.jatuh_tempo, FollowupItem.status)
        .where(FollowupItem.status != STATUS_ARSIP)
    ).all()
    servis_due = 0
    prospek = 0
    berkendala_prospek = 0
    hari_ini = 0
    aktif = 0
    akan_datang_servis = 0
    akan_datang_garansi = 0
    akan_datang_prospek = 0
    for jenis, jatuh_tempo, status in rows:
        if status in {STATUS_SELESAI, STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG}:
            continue
        aktif += 1
        if status == STATUS_AKAN_DATANG:
            if jenis == JENIS_SERVIS:
                akan_datang_servis += 1
            elif jenis == JENIS_GARANSI:
                akan_datang_garansi += 1
            elif jenis == JENIS_PROSPEK:
                akan_datang_prospek += 1
                prospek += 1
            continue
        if jenis == JENIS_SERVIS:
            servis_due += 1
        if jenis == JENIS_PROSPEK:
            if status == STATUS_KENDALA:
                berkendala_prospek += 1
            else:
                prospek += 1
        if jatuh_tempo is not None and jatuh_tempo <= day:
            hari_ini += 1
    return {
        "servis_berkala_due": servis_due,
        "prospek_count": prospek,
        "berkendala_prospek": berkendala_prospek,
        "followup_hari_ini": hari_ini,
        "followup_aktif": aktif,
        "akan_datang_servis": akan_datang_servis,
        "akan_datang_garansi": akan_datang_garansi,
        "akan_datang_prospek": akan_datang_prospek,
    }


def serialize_item(
    db: Session,
    item: FollowupItem,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
    source: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    day = today or date.today()
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    if source is None:
        ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
        sources = _collect_sources(db, day, ops)
        source = sources.get((item.jenis, item.customer_id))
    else:
        ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
    return _serialize(item, source, ops, day, moment)


def _get_item(db: Session, item_id: int) -> FollowupItem:
    item = db.get(FollowupItem, item_id)
    if item is None:
        raise NotFoundError("Item follow-up tidak ditemukan.")
    return item


def _pekerjaan(record: Optional[ServiceRecord]) -> str:
    if record is None:
        return "servis"
    return _label_acuan(record)


def _garansi_clause(record: Optional[ServiceRecord], today: date) -> str:
    if record is None or not record.bergaransi or record.garansi_sampai is None:
        return ""
    days = (record.garansi_sampai - today).days
    if days < 0:
        return "Masa garansi sudah berakhir. "
    if days <= 7:
        return (
            f"Garansi akan berakhir pada {_format_date(record.garansi_sampai)} "
            f"({days} hari lagi). "
        )
    return f"Garansi berlaku sampai {_format_date(record.garansi_sampai)}. "


def _gejala(record: Optional[ServiceRecord]) -> str:
    text = (_pekerjaan(record) or "").lower()
    if "radiator" in text:
        return "kebocoran atau suhu mesin"
    if "oli" in text:
        return "suara mesin atau tenaga"
    return "keluhan"


def _message(db: Session, item: FollowupItem, today: date) -> str:
    info = settings_service.get_bengkel_info(db)
    customer = item.customer
    record = item.service_record
    unit = describe_unit(item.unit) if item.unit is not None else "unit"
    pekerjaan = _pekerjaan(record)
    tanggal = _format_date(record.waktu_service.date() if record else None)
    opening = whatsapp_service.formal_opening(info, customer.nama)

    if item.jenis == JENIS_GARANSI and record is not None:
        days = (record.garansi_sampai - today).days if record.garansi_sampai else 0
        body = (
            f"Kami ingin menginformasikan bahwa garansi servis {unit} "
            f"tanggal {tanggal} akan berakhir pada "
            f"{_format_date(record.garansi_sampai)} ({days} hari lagi). "
            f"Bagaimana kondisi {unit} setelah {pekerjaan}? Apabila masih ada "
            f"{_gejala(record)}, kami berharap Bapak/Ibu berkenan memeriksakan "
            "kembali ke bengkel kami sebelum masa garansi berakhir."
        )
        return (
            f"{opening} {body}\n\n"
            "Terima kasih atas perhatian Bapak/Ibu."
        )

    if item.jenis == JENIS_SERVIS and record is not None:
        interval = 0
        for row in list(record.jobs) + list(record.parts):
            interval = max(interval, int(getattr(row, "interval_servis_bulan", 0) or 0))
        jadwal = _format_date(item.jatuh_tempo)
        body = (
            f"Kami ingin mengingatkan bahwa servis terakhir {pekerjaan} "
            f"pada {unit} dilaksanakan tanggal {tanggal}. Sesuai jadwal servis "
            "berkala"
            + (f" {interval} bulan" if interval else "")
            + f", waktunya servis kembali sekitar {jadwal}. "
            "Kami berharap Bapak/Ibu berkenan datang untuk menjadwalkan servis."
        )
        return (
            f"{opening} {body}\n\n"
            "Terima kasih atas perhatian Bapak/Ibu."
        )

    if item.jenis == JENIS_PIUTANG:
        if record is not None:
            return whatsapp_service.receivable_wa_text(record, info)
        body = (
            f"Kami ingin mengingatkan bahwa masih terdapat sisa pembayaran "
            f"untuk {unit}. Mohon kiranya pembayaran dapat diselesaikan. "
            "Apabila pembayaran sudah dilakukan, mohon abaikan pesan ini."
        )
        return (
            f"{opening} {body}\n\n"
            "Terima kasih atas perhatian dan kerja samanya."
        )

    body = (
        f"Kami ingin menanyakan kondisi {unit} setelah servis {pekerjaan} "
        f"tanggal {tanggal}. Apakah masih ada kendala? "
        f"{_garansi_clause(record, today)}"
        "Kami berharap Bapak/Ibu berkenan menghubungi kami apabila perlu "
        "dilakukan pemeriksaan ulang."
    )
    return (
        f"{opening} {body}\n\n"
        "Terima kasih atas perhatian Bapak/Ibu."
    )


def follow_up(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    status: str,
    operator: Operator,
    catatan: Optional[str] = None,
    pesan: Optional[str] = None,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Catat upaya follow-up dan buka WhatsApp; tidak menghapus item."""
    day = today or date.today()
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    normalized = (status or "").strip().upper()
    if normalized not in whatsapp_service.VALID_STATUSES:
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Status WhatsApp harus DIBUKA, DIBATALKAN, atau TIDAK_TERSEDIA."
        )

    with db.begin():
        ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
        item = _get_item(db, item_id)
        if item.status == STATUS_ARSIP:
            raise ConflictError("Item sudah diarsipkan.")
        if item.status in {STATUS_SELESAI, STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG}:
            raise ConflictError("Item sudah ditutup.")
        if not settings_service.within_followup_window(ops, moment):
            raise ConflictError(settings_service.followup_window_message(ops))
        cooldown = int(ops.get("cooldown_hari", 7))
        if cooldown > 0 and item.followup_terakhir is not None:
            until = item.followup_terakhir + timedelta(days=cooldown)
            if moment < until:
                raise ConflictError(
                    "Follow up baru bisa diulang setelah "
                    f"{until.strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M')}."
                )

        message = (pesan or "").strip() or _message(db, item, day)
        log, link = whatsapp_service.append_attempt(
            db,
            item.customer,
            jenis=WA_JENIS.get(item.jenis, whatsapp_service.JENIS_FOLLOWUP),
            status=normalized,
            message=message,
            operator=operator,
            record=item.service_record,
            catatan=catatan,
            now=moment,
        )
        item.followup_terakhir = moment
        if (
            normalized == whatsapp_service.STATUS_OPENED
            and item.jenis == JENIS_PIUTANG
        ):
            record = item.service_record
            if (
                record is not None
                and (record.status or "NORMAL") == "NORMAL"
                and (record.status_bayar or "LUNAS") == "BELUM_LUNAS"
                and _sisa(record) > 0
            ):
                record.diingatkan_pada = moment
        if item.status in {STATUS_BARU, STATUS_SUDAH}:
            previous = item.status
            item.status = STATUS_SUDAH
            _add_event(
                db,
                item,
                previous,
                STATUS_SUDAH,
                "Follow up " + normalized.lower() + (f": {catatan}" if catatan else ""),
                operator.label,
                moment,
            )
        db.flush()

    db.refresh(item)
    return {"item": item, "wa_text": message, "link": link, "log": log}


def mark_piutang_followed(
    db: Session,
    customer_id: int,
    moment: datetime,
    operator_label: str,
    reason: str,
) -> Optional[FollowupItem]:
    """Samakan item piutang dengan pengingat yang dikirim dari jalur lain."""
    item = db.scalar(
        select(FollowupItem)
        .where(
            FollowupItem.jenis == JENIS_PIUTANG,
            FollowupItem.customer_id == customer_id,
            FollowupItem.status != STATUS_ARSIP,
        )
        .order_by(FollowupItem.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if item is None:
        return None
    item.followup_terakhir = moment
    if item.status in {STATUS_BARU, STATUS_SUDAH}:
        previous = item.status
        item.status = STATUS_SUDAH
        _add_event(db, item, previous, STATUS_SUDAH, reason, operator_label, moment)
    db.flush()
    return item


def change_status(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    status: str,
    operator: Operator,
    alasan: Optional[str] = None,
    janji_bayar: Optional[date] = None,
    tanggal_service: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> FollowupItem:
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    target = (status or "").strip().upper()
    if target not in STATUS_VALUES:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Status follow-up tidak dikenal.")

    with db.begin():
        item = _get_item(db, item_id)
        if item.status == STATUS_ARSIP:
            raise ConflictError("Item arsip tidak dapat diubah.")
        if item.jenis in JADWAL_JENIS:
            if target not in {
                STATUS_AKAN_DATANG,
                STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG,
                STATUS_SELESAI,
                STATUS_ARSIP,
            }:
                raise BusinessValidationError(
                    "Status servis berkala dan garansi hanya akan datang "
                    "service, datang, tidak akan datang service, atau dihapus "
                    "dari daftar."
                )
            if target == STATUS_AKAN_DATANG and tanggal_service is None:
                raise BusinessValidationError("Tanggal service wajib diisi.")
            if target != STATUS_ARSIP and item.followup_terakhir is None:
                raise ConflictError(
                    "Lakukan follow up WhatsApp dulu sebelum mengubah status."
                )
        elif item.jenis == JENIS_PROSPEK:
            if target != STATUS_ARSIP and item.followup_terakhir is None:
                raise ConflictError(
                    "Lakukan follow up WhatsApp dulu sebelum mengubah status."
                )
            if target == STATUS_AKAN_DATANG and tanggal_service is None:
                raise BusinessValidationError("Tanggal kunjungan wajib diisi.")
        elif target in {STATUS_AKAN_DATANG, STATUS_TIDAK_DATANG}:
            raise BusinessValidationError(
                "Status akan/tidak datang service hanya untuk prospek, "
                "servis berkala, dan garansi."
            )
        if target == item.status and item.jenis not in JADWAL_JENIS:
            if not (
                item.jenis == JENIS_PROSPEK
                and target == STATUS_AKAN_DATANG
                and tanggal_service is not None
            ):
                raise BusinessValidationError("Status item sudah sama.")
        if item.jenis == JENIS_PIUTANG and target in {STATUS_SELESAI, STATUS_ARSIP}:
            raise BusinessValidationError(
                "Piutang hanya keluar dari daftar lewat pelunasan penuh atau void."
            )
        if target == STATUS_JANJI and item.jenis != JENIS_PIUTANG:
            raise BusinessValidationError("Janji bayar hanya untuk piutang.")
        if target == STATUS_JANJI and janji_bayar is None:
            raise BusinessValidationError("Tanggal janji bayar wajib diisi.")
        if target == STATUS_ARSIP and not (alasan or "").strip():
            raise BusinessValidationError("Alasan arsip wajib diisi.")

        previous = item.status
        item.status = target
        item.tanggal_service = (
            tanggal_service if target == STATUS_AKAN_DATANG else None
        )
        if target == STATUS_JANJI:
            item.janji_bayar = janji_bayar
        else:
            item.janji_bayar = None
        if target == STATUS_ARSIP:
            item.alasan_arsip = (alasan or "").strip()
            item.archived_at = moment
        _add_event(db, item, previous, target, alasan, operator.label, moment)
        db.flush()

    db.refresh(item)
    return item


def _last_sent_message(db: Session, item: FollowupItem) -> str:
    jenis = WA_JENIS.get(item.jenis, whatsapp_service.JENIS_FOLLOWUP)
    log = db.scalar(
        select(WhatsAppLog)
        .where(
            WhatsAppLog.customer_id == item.customer_id,
            WhatsAppLog.jenis == jenis,
            WhatsAppLog.status == whatsapp_service.STATUS_OPENED,
            WhatsAppLog.created_at >= item.created_at,
        )
        .order_by(WhatsAppLog.created_at.desc(), WhatsAppLog.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    return (log.pesan or "").strip() if log is not None else ""


def detail(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    today: Optional[date] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    day = today or date.today()
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    sources = sync_items(db, today=day, now=moment)
    item = _get_item(db, item_id)
    ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
    event_rows = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(FollowupEvent)
            .where(FollowupEvent.item_id == item.id)
            .order_by(FollowupEvent.created_at.asc(), FollowupEvent.id.asc())
        ).all()
    )
    return {
        "item": _serialize(
            item, sources.get((item.jenis, item.customer_id)), ops, day, moment
        ),
        "events": event_rows,
        "pesan": _last_sent_message(db, item) or _message(db, item, day),
    }


==============================================================================

"""Pelunasan bertahap, refund, dan daftar piutang (Tahap 11)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from database.models import (
    Customer,
    ServicePayment,
    ServiceRecord,
    VehicleOrUnit,
)
from database.session import begin_immediate
from services import audit_service, settings_service
from services.auth_service import Operator
from services.errors import (
    BusinessValidationError,
    ConflictError,
    NotFoundError,
    PaymentError,
)
from services.utils import clean_text, money


def record_netto(record: ServiceRecord) -> Decimal:
    if (record.status or "NORMAL") == "VOID":
        return money(Decimal("0.00"))
    return money(
        max(
            Decimal(record.total_servis or 0)
            - Decimal(record.total_retur or 0),
            Decimal("0.00"),
        )
    )


def record_sisa(record: ServiceRecord) -> Decimal:
    if (record.status or "NORMAL") == "VOID":
        return money(Decimal("0.00"))
    return money(
        max(record_netto(record) - Decimal(record.total_dibayar or 0), Decimal("0.00"))
    )


def list_payments(db: Session, record_id: int) -> List[ServicePayment]:
    if db.get(ServiceRecord, record_id) is None:
        raise NotFoundError("Nota servis tidak ditemukan.")
    statement = (
        select(ServicePayment)
        .where(ServicePayment.service_record_id == record_id)
        .order_by(ServicePayment.waktu.asc(), ServicePayment.id.asc())
    )
    return list(db.scalars(statement).all())


def record_payment(
    db: Session,
    record_id: int,
    nominal: Any,
    metode: str,
    operator: Operator,
    catatan: Optional[str] = None,
    request_id: Optional[str] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Tuple[ServicePayment, ServiceRecord, bool]:
    """Catat pelunasan atomik. Mengembalikan (payment, record, created)."""
    request_key = (request_id or "").strip() or None
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    method = (metode or "").strip().upper()
    if method not in {"CASH", "TRANSFER"}:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Metode pembayaran harus CASH atau TRANSFER.")
    value = money(nominal)
    if value <= 0:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Nominal pelunasan harus lebih dari Rp 0.")
    note = clean_text(catatan, max_length=500) or None

    try:
        with db.begin():
            begin_immediate(db)
            if request_key:
                existing = db.scalar(
                    select(ServicePayment).where(
                        ServicePayment.request_id == request_key
                    )
                )
                if existing is not None:
                    if existing.service_record_id != record_id:
                        raise ConflictError(
                            "Request pelunasan sudah dipakai untuk nota lain."
                        )
                    record = db.get(ServiceRecord, record_id)
                    return existing, record, False

            record = db.get(ServiceRecord, record_id)
            if record is None:
                raise NotFoundError("Nota servis tidak ditemukan.")
            if (record.status or "NORMAL") == "VOID":
                raise ConflictError("Nota sudah dibatalkan (VOID).")
            sisa = record_sisa(record)
            if sisa <= 0 or (record.status_bayar or "LUNAS") == "LUNAS":
                raise ConflictError("Nota ini sudah lunas.")
            if value > sisa:
                raise PaymentError(
                    f"Nominal melebihi sisa piutang ({sisa:.2f})."
                )

            payment = ServicePayment(
                service_record_id=record.id,
                jenis="PELUNASAN",
                metode=method,
                nominal=value,
                waktu=moment,
                operator=operator.label,
                catatan=note,
                request_id=request_key,
                created_at=moment,
            )
            db.add(payment)

            total_dibayar = money(Decimal(record.total_dibayar or 0) + value)
            record.total_dibayar = total_dibayar
            record.status_bayar = (
                "LUNAS" if record_sisa(record) <= 0 else "BELUM_LUNAS"
            )
            if record.status_bayar == "BELUM_LUNAS":
                record.jenis_bayar = "KREDIT"

            audit_service.record_event(
                db,
                operator,
                audit_service.ACTION_PAYMENT,
                audit_service.REFERENCE_SERVICE_RECORD,
                reference_id=record.no_nota,
                reason=f"Pelunasan {method} Rp {value:.2f}",
                now=moment,
            )
            db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:
        db.rollback()
        if request_key:
            existing = db.scalar(
                select(ServicePayment).where(ServicePayment.request_id == request_key)
            )
            if existing is not None:
                return existing, db.get(ServiceRecord, record_id), False
        raise

    return payment, record, True


def refund_void(
    db: Session, record: ServiceRecord, operator_label: str, reason: str, moment: datetime
) -> Optional[ServicePayment]:
    """Kembalikan seluruh uang yang sudah diterima saat nota di-void."""
    paid = money(record.total_dibayar or 0)
    if paid <= 0:
        record.total_dibayar = money(Decimal("0.00"))
        record.status_bayar = "LUNAS"
        return None
    refund = ServicePayment(
        service_record_id=record.id,
        jenis="REFUND",
        metode=record.metode_bayar or "CASH",
        nominal=money(-paid),
        waktu=moment,
        operator=operator_label,
        catatan=f"Refund void {record.no_nota}: {reason}",
        created_at=moment,
    )
    db.add(refund)
    record.total_dibayar = money(Decimal("0.00"))
    record.status_bayar = "LUNAS"
    return refund


def refund_excess(
    db: Session,
    record: ServiceRecord,
    operator_label: str,
    reason: str,
    moment: datetime,
    netto: Optional[Decimal] = None,
) -> Optional[ServicePayment]:
    """Kembalikan kelebihan bayar setelah retur; tidak pernah negatif."""
    target = money(record_netto(record) if netto is None else netto)
    paid = money(record.total_dibayar or 0)
    refunded: Optional[ServicePayment] = None
    if paid > target:
        excess = money(paid - target)
        refunded = ServicePayment(
            service_record_id=record.id,
            jenis="REFUND",
            metode=record.metode_bayar or "CASH",
            nominal=money(-excess),
            waktu=moment,
            operator=operator_label,
            catatan=f"Refund retur {record.no_nota}: {reason}",
            created_at=moment,
        )
        db.add(refunded)
        paid = target
    record.total_dibayar = money(paid)
    record.status_bayar = "LUNAS" if money(target - paid) <= 0 else "BELUM_LUNAS"
    return refunded


def reschedule_due_date(
    db: Session,
    record_id: int,
    jatuh_tempo: date,
    operator: Operator,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> ServiceRecord:
    """Pindahkan jatuh tempo piutang dan buka lagi pengingat untuk siklus baru."""
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    if jatuh_tempo < moment.date():
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Tanggal jatuh tempo tidak boleh di masa lalu."
        )
    with db.begin():
        record = db.get(ServiceRecord, record_id)
        if record is None:
            raise NotFoundError("Nota servis tidak ditemukan.")
        if (record.status or "NORMAL") == "VOID":
            raise ConflictError("Nota sudah dibatalkan (VOID).")
        if (
            (record.status_bayar or "LUNAS") == "LUNAS"
            or record_sisa(record) <= 0
        ):
            raise ConflictError("Nota ini sudah lunas.")
        if record.jatuh_tempo == jatuh_tempo:
            raise BusinessValidationError("Tanggal jatuh tempo tidak berubah.")
        previous = record.jatuh_tempo
        record.jatuh_tempo = jatuh_tempo
        record.diingatkan_pada = None
        audit_service.record_event(
            db,
            operator,
            audit_service.ACTION_RESCHEDULE,
            audit_service.REFERENCE_SERVICE_RECORD,
            reference_id=record.no_nota,
            reason=(
                f"Jatuh tempo {previous.isoformat() if previous else '-'} "
                f"menjadi {jatuh_tempo.isoformat()}"
            ),
            now=moment,
        )
        db.flush()
    db.refresh(record)
    return record


def _age_bucket(days: int) -> str:
    if days <= 7:
        return "0-7"
    if days <= 30:
        return "8-30"
    if days <= 60:
        return "31-60"
    return ">60"


def _unpaid_records(
    db: Session, today: Optional[date] = None, customer_id: Optional[int] = None
) -> List[ServiceRecord]:
    statement = (
        select(ServiceRecord)
        .join(VehicleOrUnit, ServiceRecord.unit_id == VehicleOrUnit.id)
        .join(Customer, VehicleOrUnit.customer_id == Customer.id)
        .options(
            selectinload(ServiceRecord.unit).selectinload(VehicleOrUnit.customer)
        )
        .where(
            ServiceRecord.status == "NORMAL",
            ServiceRecord.status_bayar == "BELUM_LUNAS",
        )
        .order_by(ServiceRecord.jatuh_tempo.asc(), ServiceRecord.waktu_service.asc())
    )
    if customer_id is not None:
        statement = statement.where(Customer.id == customer_id)
    records = list(db.scalars(statement).all())
    return [record for record in records if record_sisa(record) > 0]


def receivables(
    db: Session, today: Optional[date] = None, customer_id: Optional[int] = None
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    day = today or date.today()
    ops = settings_service.get_ops_settings(db)
    window_open = settings_service.within_followup_window(ops)
    groups: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for record in _unpaid_records(db, day, customer_id):
        customer = record.unit.customer
        group = groups.get(customer.id)
        if group is None:
            group = {
                "customer_id": customer.id,
                "nama": customer.nama,
                "nomor_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
                "jumlah_nota": 0,
                "total_sisa": Decimal("0.00"),
                "jatuh_tempo_terdekat": None,
                "umur_tertua_hari": 0,
                "records": [],
            }
            groups[customer.id] = group

        netto = record_netto(record)
        sisa = record_sisa(record)
        age = max((day - record.waktu_service.date()).days, 0)
        overdue = bool(record.jatuh_tempo and record.jatuh_tempo < day)
        sudah_diingatkan = record.diingatkan_pada is not None
        tempo_tiba = record.jatuh_tempo is None or record.jatuh_tempo <= day
        group["jumlah_nota"] += 1
        group["total_sisa"] += sisa
        group["umur_tertua_hari"] = max(group["umur_tertua_hari"], age)
        if record.jatuh_tempo is not None:
            current = group["jatuh_tempo_terdekat"]
            if current is None or record.jatuh_tempo < current:
                group["jatuh_tempo_terdekat"] = record.jatuh_tempo
        group["records"].append(
            {
                "id": record.id,
                "no_nota": record.no_nota,
                "waktu_service": record.waktu_service,
                "metode_bayar": record.metode_bayar,
                "total_servis": record.total_servis,
                "total_neto": netto,
                "total_dibayar": money(record.total_dibayar or 0),
                "sisa_bayar": sisa,
                "jatuh_tempo": record.jatuh_tempo,
                "diingatkan_pada": record.diingatkan_pada,
                "sudah_diingatkan": sudah_diingatkan,
                "bisa_diingatkan": bool(
                    tempo_tiba and not sudah_diingatkan and window_open
                ),
                "umur_hari": age,
                "umur_bucket": _age_bucket(age),
                "overdue": overdue,
            }
        )

    items = sorted(
        groups.values(),
        key=lambda item: (-item["total_sisa"], item["nama"].lower()),
    )
    for item in items:
        item["total_sisa"] = money(item["total_sisa"])
        item["records"].sort(
            key=lambda row: (row["jatuh_tempo"] or date.max, row["waktu_service"])
        )
    return {
        "jumlah_pelanggan": len(items),
        "jumlah_nota": sum(item["jumlah_nota"] for item in items),
        "total_sisa": money(sum((item["total_sisa"] for item in items), Decimal("0.00"))),
        "items": items,
    }


==============================================================================

"""Identitas bengkel yang dapat diubah dari aplikasi.

Nilai default berasal dari `.env`; perubahan dari UI disimpan di tabel
`app_settings` dan menimpa default tersebut.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from config import settings
from database.models import AppSetting
from services.errors import BusinessValidationError
from services.utils import clean_text

# field payload -> atribut Settings (nilai default dari .env)
FIELD_TO_ENV = {
    "nama": "bengkel_nama",
    "tagline": "bengkel_tagline",
    "alamat": "bengkel_alamat",
    "telepon": "bengkel_telepon",
    "wa": "bengkel_wa",
    "bank_nama": "bengkel_bank_nama",
    "no_rekening": "bengkel_no_rekening",
    "atas_nama": "bengkel_atas_nama",
}

FIELD_MAX_LENGTH = {
    "nama": 255,
    "tagline": 255,
    "alamat": 500,
    "telepon": 64,
    "wa": 64,
    "bank_nama": 255,
    "no_rekening": 64,
    "atas_nama": 255,
}

KEY_PREFIX = "bengkel."

TELEGRAM_PREFIX = "telegram."
BACKUP_STALE_DAYS = 2
BACKUP_LAST_AT_KEY = "backup.last_local_at"
BACKUP_LAST_FILENAME_KEY = "backup.last_local_filename"
BACKUP_LAST_STATUS_KEY = "backup.last_status"
BACKUP_LAST_ERROR_KEY = "backup.last_error"
BACKUP_LAST_TELEGRAM_KEY = "backup.last_telegram_at"
_TIME_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")
_BOT_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^\d+:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}$")
_CHAT_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^(-?\d+|@[A-Za-z0-9_]{4,})$")


def default_bengkel_info() -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Identitas dari `.env` saja (tanpa akses database)."""
    return {field: getattr(settings, attr) for field, attr in FIELD_TO_ENV.items()}


def get_bengkel_info(db: Session) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Gabungkan default .env dengan nilai yang tersimpan di database."""
    info = default_bengkel_info()
    stored = db.scalars(
        select(AppSetting).where(AppSetting.key.like(f"{KEY_PREFIX}%"))
    ).all()
    for row in stored:
        field = row.key[len(KEY_PREFIX):]
        if field in info:
            info[field] = row.value
    return info


def set_setting(db: Session, key: str, value: str) -> None:
    row = db.get(AppSetting, key)
    if row is None:
        db.add(AppSetting(key=key, value=value))
    else:
        row.value = value


def get_setting(db: Session, key: str, default: str = "") -> str:
    row = db.get(AppSetting, key)
    return row.value if row is not None else default


def update_bengkel_info(db: Session, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
    cleaned = {
        field: clean_text(payload.get(field), max_length=FIELD_MAX_LENGTH[field])
        for field in FIELD_TO_ENV
    }
    if len(cleaned["nama"]) < 2:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Nama bengkel minimal 2 karakter.")

    for field, value in cleaned.items():
        set_setting(db, f"{KEY_PREFIX}{field}", value)

    db.commit()
    return get_bengkel_info(db)


OPS_PREFIX = "ops."
OPS_INT_KEYS = {
    "tempo_hari": (1, 365, 7),
    "prospek_hari": (1, 365, 7),
    "cooldown_hari": (0, 90, 7),
    "garansi_akan_habis_hari": (1, 90, 7),
    "servis_berkala_awal_hari": (1, 90, 14),
}
OPS_TIME_KEYS = {
    "followup_jam_mulai": "08:00",
    "followup_jam_selesai": "20:00",
}
OPS_TIME_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$")


def get_ops_settings(db: Session) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Pengaturan operasional Tahap 11-13 (pembayaran, follow-up, WhatsApp)."""
    stored = {
        row.key[len(OPS_PREFIX):]: row.value
        for row in db.scalars(
            select(AppSetting).where(AppSetting.key.like(f"{OPS_PREFIX}%"))
        ).all()
    }
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, (_low, _high, default) in OPS_INT_KEYS.items():
        raw = stored.get(key, "")
        try:
            result[key] = int(raw) if raw != "" else default
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            result[key] = default
    for key, default in OPS_TIME_KEYS.items():
        raw = stored.get(key, "")
        result[key] = raw if OPS_TIME_PATTERN.match(raw or "") else default
    return result


def _time_minutes(value: str, fallback: int) -> int:
    if not OPS_TIME_PATTERN.match(value or ""):
        return fallback
    hour, minute = value.split(":")
    return int(hour) * 60 + int(minute)


def followup_window(ops: Dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    return (
        _time_minutes(ops.get("followup_jam_mulai", "08:00"), 8 * 60),
        _time_minutes(ops.get("followup_jam_selesai", "20:00"), 20 * 60),
    )


def followup_window_label(ops: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    start, end = followup_window(ops)

    def fmt(minutes: int) -> str:
        return f"{minutes // 60:02d}.{minutes % 60:02d}"

    return f"{fmt(start)}-{fmt(end)}"


def within_followup_window(
    ops: Dict[str, Any], moment: Optional[datetime] = None
) -> bool:
    start, end = followup_window(ops)
    if start == end:
        return True
    current = moment or datetime.now()
    minutes = current.hour * 60 + current.minute
    if start < end:
        return start <= minutes < end
    return minutes >= start or minutes < end


def followup_window_message(ops: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        "Follow up hanya bisa dilakukan antara pukul "
        f"{followup_window_label(ops)}."
    )


def update_ops_settings(db: Session, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    current = get_ops_settings(db)
    cleaned: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for key, (low, high, _default) in OPS_INT_KEYS.items():
        raw = payload.get(key, current[key])
        try:
            value = int(raw)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise BusinessValidationError(f"Nilai {key} harus berupa angka.")
        if not low <= value <= high:
            raise BusinessValidationError(
                f"Nilai {key} harus antara {low} dan {high}."
            )
        cleaned[f"{OPS_PREFIX}{key}"] = str(value)
    for key, default in OPS_TIME_KEYS.items():
        raw = payload.get(key, current[key])
        value = str(raw if raw is not None else default).strip()
        if not OPS_TIME_PATTERN.match(value):
            raise BusinessValidationError(
                f"Nilai {key} harus berformat HH:MM."
            )
        cleaned[f"{OPS_PREFIX}{key}"] = value
    if (
        cleaned[f"{OPS_PREFIX}followup_jam_mulai"]
        == cleaned[f"{OPS_PREFIX}followup_jam_selesai"]
    ):
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Jam mulai dan jam selesai follow up tidak boleh sama."
        )
    for key, value in cleaned.items():
        set_setting(db, key, value)
    db.commit()
    return get_ops_settings(db)


def _stored_values(db: Session) -> Dict[str, str]:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(AppSetting).where(AppSetting.key.like(f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}%"))
    ).all()
    return {row.key[len(TELEGRAM_PREFIX):]: row.value for row in rows}


def _stored_bool(value: Any, default: bool = False) -> bool:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return value
    text = str(value if value is not None else "").strip().lower()
    if not text:
        return default
    return text in {"1", "true", "yes", "on", "ya"}


def get_telegram_config(db: Session) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Konfigurasi bot Telegram (default .env, ditimpa nilai dari UI)."""
    stored = _stored_values(db)
    enabled_default = settings.telegram_backup_enabled
    jam = stored.get("jam") or settings.telegram_backup_time or "21:00"
    return {
        "bot_token": stored.get("bot_token") or settings.telegram_bot_token,
        "chat_id": stored.get("chat_id") or settings.telegram_chat_id,
        "enabled": _stored_bool(stored.get("enabled"), enabled_default),
        "jam": jam,
        "needs_reconfig": _stored_bool(stored.get("needs_reconfig")),
        "last_sent_at": stored.get("last_sent_at", ""),
        "last_attempt_at": stored.get("last_attempt_at", ""),
        "pending_since": stored.get("pending_since", ""),
        "last_error": stored.get("last_error", ""),
    }


def telegram_public_info(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Data aman ditampilkan ke UI (tanpa bot token)."""
    return {
        "enabled": bool(config.get("enabled")),
        "jam": config.get("jam") or "21:00",
        "chat_id": config.get("chat_id") or "",
        "configured": bool(config.get("bot_token") and config.get("chat_id")),
        "needs_reconfig": bool(config.get("needs_reconfig")),
        "last_sent_at": config.get("last_sent_at") or None,
        "last_error": config.get("last_error") or None,
    }


def _parse_iso(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime]:
    try:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(str(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def get_backup_status(
    db: Session,
    config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Status backup terakhir + penanda basi untuk peringatan di UI.

    Backup manual Telegram dari versi sebelumnya tidak menulis kunci
    `backup.*`; nilainya jatuh ke `telegram.last_sent_at` agar pengguna lama
    tidak langsung melihat peringatan palsu.
    """
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    telegram = config if config is not None else get_telegram_config(db)
    last_at = (
        get_setting(db, BACKUP_LAST_AT_KEY)
        or telegram.get("last_sent_at")
        or ""
    )
    last_dt = _parse_iso(last_at)
    stale = last_dt is None or moment - last_dt > timedelta(days=BACKUP_STALE_DAYS)
    return {
        "last_local_at": last_at or None,
        "last_local_filename": get_setting(db, BACKUP_LAST_FILENAME_KEY) or None,
        "last_status": get_setting(db, BACKUP_LAST_STATUS_KEY) or None,
        "last_error": get_setting(db, BACKUP_LAST_ERROR_KEY) or None,
        "last_telegram_at": (
            get_setting(db, BACKUP_LAST_TELEGRAM_KEY)
            or telegram.get("last_sent_at")
            or None
        ),
        "stale": stale,
        "stale_days": BACKUP_STALE_DAYS,
    }


def update_telegram_config(db: Session, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    enabled = bool(payload.get("enabled"))
    jam = clean_text(payload.get("jam"), max_length=5) or "21:00"
    if not _TIME_PATTERN.match(jam):
        raise BusinessValidationError("Jam backup harus berformat HH:MM (contoh 21:00).")

    token = clean_text(payload.get("bot_token"), max_length=200)
    chat_id = clean_text(payload.get("chat_id"), max_length=64)
    if token and not _BOT_TOKEN_PATTERN.match(token):
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Format bot token Telegram tidak valid (contoh 123456789:AA...)."
        )
    if chat_id and not _CHAT_ID_PATTERN.match(chat_id):
        raise BusinessValidationError(
            "Format chat ID Telegram tidak valid (contoh 123456789 atau @namachannel)."
        )

    # Token/chat ID kosong berarti pertahankan nilai yang sudah tersimpan.
    if token:
        set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}bot_token", token)
    if chat_id:
        set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}chat_id", chat_id)
    set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}enabled", "1" if enabled else "0")
    set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}jam", jam)
    set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}last_error", "")
    db.commit()

    config = get_telegram_config(db)
    if config["bot_token"] and config["chat_id"]:
        set_setting(db, f"{TELEGRAM_PREFIX}needs_reconfig", "")
        db.commit()
        config = get_telegram_config(db)
    return config

FILE: services/pengerjaan_service.py  (132 baris)
==============================================================================

"""Follow-up kendaraan selesai untuk nota dengan penanda unit ditinggal.

Nota yang dicatat dengan opsi "Unit ditinggal" membuat satu baris di
`pengerjaan_nota`. Modul ini membaca baris tersebut sebagai daftar unit yang
masih di bengkel, dipakai kasir untuk mengabari pelanggan saat pengerjaan
selesai, lalu menutupnya ketika unit sudah diambil.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload

from database.models import (
    PengerjaanNota,
    ServiceRecord,
    VehicleOrUnit,
    WhatsAppLog,
)
from services import payment_service
from services.auth_service import Operator
from services.customer_service import describe_unit
from services.errors import ConflictError, NotFoundError

STATUS_DIKERJAKAN = "SEDANG_DIKERJAKAN"
STATUS_DIAMBIL = "SUDAH_DIAMBIL"


def _statement():
    return (
        select(PengerjaanNota)
        .join(ServiceRecord, PengerjaanNota.service_record_id == ServiceRecord.id)
        .where(
            PengerjaanNota.diambil_pada.is_(None),
            ServiceRecord.status == "NORMAL",
        )
        .options(
            joinedload(PengerjaanNota.service_record)
            .joinedload(ServiceRecord.unit)
            .joinedload(VehicleOrUnit.customer)
        )
    )


def serialize_item(marker: PengerjaanNota) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    record = marker.service_record
    customer = record.unit.customer
    return {
        "id": marker.id,
        "record_id": record.id,
        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
        "waktu_service": record.waktu_service,
        "customer_id": customer.id,
        "customer_nama": customer.nama,
        "nomor_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
        "unit_deskripsi": describe_unit(record.unit),
        "total_servis": record.total_servis,
        "sisa_bayar": payment_service.record_sisa(record),
        "status_pengerjaan": marker.status_pengerjaan,
        "janji_ambil": marker.janji_ambil,
        "dibuat_pada": marker.dibuat_pada,
        "diambil_pada": marker.diambil_pada,
    }


def list_items(db: Session) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Unit yang masih di bengkel, yang paling lama menunggu lebih dulu."""
    markers = list(
        db.scalars(_statement().order_by(PengerjaanNota.dibuat_pada.asc())).unique()
    )
    record_ids = [marker.service_record_id for marker in markers]
    notified: Dict[int, datetime] = {}
    if record_ids:
        rows = db.execute(
            select(
                WhatsAppLog.service_record_id,
                func.max(WhatsAppLog.created_at),
            )
            .where(
                WhatsAppLog.service_record_id.in_(record_ids),
                WhatsAppLog.jenis == "piutang",
                WhatsAppLog.status == "DIBUKA",
            )
            .group_by(WhatsAppLog.service_record_id)
        ).all()
        notified = {row[0]: row[1] for row in rows}
    items = []
    for marker in markers:
        item = serialize_item(marker)
        dikabari_pada = notified.get(marker.service_record_id)
        if (
            dikabari_pada is not None
            and marker.dibuat_pada is not None
            and dikabari_pada < marker.dibuat_pada
        ):
            dikabari_pada = None
        item["dikabari_pada"] = dikabari_pada
        item["sudah_dikabari"] = dikabari_pada is not None
        items.append(item)
    return {"items": items, "jumlah": len(items)}


def counts(db: Session) -> Dict[str, int]:
    return {"kendaraan_selesai": len(list(db.scalars(_statement()).unique()))}


def mark_taken(
    db: Session,
    marker_id: int,
    operator: Operator,
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> PengerjaanNota:
    """Tutup penanda setelah pelanggan mengambil unit."""
    moment = now or datetime.now()
    with db.begin():
        marker = db.get(PengerjaanNota, marker_id)
        if marker is None:
            raise NotFoundError("Penanda pengerjaan tidak ditemukan.")
        if marker.diambil_pada is not None:
            raise ConflictError("Unit sudah ditandai diambil.")
        if (marker.service_record.status or "NORMAL") == "VOID":
            raise ConflictError("Nota sudah dibatalkan.")
        marker.diambil_pada = moment
        marker.status_pengerjaan = STATUS_DIAMBIL
        marker.diubah_pada = moment
        marker.diubah_oleh = operator.label
        db.flush()
    db.refresh(marker)
    return marker

FILE: services/customer_service.py  (191 baris)

"""Logika bisnis pelanggan & unit servis (mobil / radiator copotan)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import List, Optional

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from database.models import Customer, VehicleOrUnit
from database.session import begin_immediate
from repositories import customer_repository as repo
from services import audit_service
from services.auth_service import Operator
from services.errors import BusinessValidationError, NotFoundError
from services.utils import clean_text, normalize_nopol, normalize_phone

VALID_UNIT_TYPES = {"MOBIL", "RADIATOR_ONLY"}


def create_customer(
    db: Session,
    nama: str,
    nomor_hp: str,
    alamat: Optional[str] = None,
) -> Customer:
    clean_nama = clean_text(nama, max_length=255, allow_newlines=False)
    if len(clean_nama) < 2:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Nama pelanggan minimal 2 karakter.")

    phone = normalize_phone(nomor_hp)
    if not (8 <= len(phone) <= 15):
        raise BusinessValidationError("Nomor HP tidak valid (8-15 digit).")

    clean_alamat = clean_text(alamat, max_length=500) or None
    return repo.create_customer(db, clean_nama, phone, clean_alamat)


def archive_unit(
    db: Session,
    unit_id: int,
    operator: Optional[Operator] = None,
) -> VehicleOrUnit:
    """Soft delete unit: sembunyikan dari pilihan tanpa memutus riwayat nota."""
    with db.begin():
        begin_immediate(db)
        unit = repo.get_unit(db, unit_id)
        if unit is None:
            raise NotFoundError("Unit servis tidak ditemukan.")
        if unit.archived_at is not None:
            db.flush()
        else:
            unit.archived_at = datetime.now()
            if operator is not None:
                audit_service.record_event(
                    db,
                    operator,
                    audit_service.ACTION_UNIT,
                    audit_service.REFERENCE_UNIT,
                    reference_id=str(unit.id),
                    reason="Unit dihapus dari daftar",
                )
            db.flush()
    db.refresh(unit)
    return unit


def search_customers(db: Session, query: str = "", limit: int = 20) -> List[Customer]:
    return repo.search_customers(db, query, limit)


def list_customers_with_units(
    db: Session, query: str = "", limit: int = 100
) -> List[Customer]:
    return repo.list_customers_with_units(db, query, limit)


def list_units(db: Session, customer_id: int) -> List[VehicleOrUnit]:
    if repo.get_customer(db, customer_id) is None:
        raise NotFoundError("Pelanggan tidak ditemukan.")
    return repo.list_units(db, customer_id)


def _next_radiator_label(db: Session, today: date) -> str:
    """Generator label fisik unik RAD-YYYYMMDD-XXX.

    Ref: PRD FR-UNIT-04 / TC-05.
    """
    prefix = f"RAD-{today:%Y%m%d}-"
    highest = 0
    for label in repo.labels_with_prefix(db, prefix):
        suffix = label[len(prefix):]
        if suffix.isdigit():
            highest = max(highest, int(suffix))
    return f"{prefix}{highest + 1:03d}"


def ensure_unit_label(db: Session, unit: VehicleOrUnit, today: date) -> str:
    """Buat label barang saat transaksi pertama, lalu pakai ulang selamanya.

    Label lama tidak pernah diganti. Pemanggil harus sudah memegang kunci tulis
    (BEGIN IMMEDIATE) agar urutan harian tidak bertabrakan.
    """
    if unit.label_radiator:
        return unit.label_radiator
    unit.label_radiator = _next_radiator_label(db, today)
    db.flush()
    return unit.label_radiator


def create_unit(
    db: Session,
    customer_id: int,
    unit_type: str,
    nopol: Optional[str] = None,
    jenis_mobil: Optional[str] = None,
    tahun_mobil: Optional[int] = None,
    tipe_radiator: Optional[str] = None,
    keterangan: Optional[str] = None,
) -> VehicleOrUnit:
    normalized_type = (unit_type or "").strip().upper()
    if normalized_type not in VALID_UNIT_TYPES:
        raise BusinessValidationError("Jenis unit harus MOBIL atau RADIATOR_ONLY.")

    if tahun_mobil is not None and not (1900 <= int(tahun_mobil) <= 2100):
        raise BusinessValidationError("Tahun mobil tidak valid.")

    clean_jenis = clean_text(jenis_mobil, max_length=255, allow_newlines=False) or None
    clean_tipe = clean_text(tipe_radiator, max_length=255, allow_newlines=False) or None
    clean_ket = clean_text(keterangan, max_length=1000) or None

    if normalized_type == "MOBIL":
        normalized_nopol = normalize_nopol(nopol)
        if not normalized_nopol:
            raise BusinessValidationError("Nomor polisi wajib diisi untuk unit mobil.")
        if len(normalized_nopol) > 32:
            raise BusinessValidationError("Nomor polisi terlalu panjang.")
        with db.begin():
            begin_immediate(db)
            if repo.get_customer(db, customer_id) is None:
                raise NotFoundError("Pelanggan tidak ditemukan.")
            unit = VehicleOrUnit(
                customer_id=customer_id,
                unit_type="MOBIL",
                nopol=normalized_nopol,
                jenis_mobil=clean_jenis,
                tahun_mobil=int(tahun_mobil) if tahun_mobil is not None else None,
                label_radiator=None,
                tipe_radiator=None,
                keterangan=clean_ket,
            )
            db.add(unit)
            db.flush()
        db.refresh(unit)
        return unit

    # Ref: PRD FR-UNIT-04 - radiator copotan tanpa nopol. Label fisik baru
    # dibuat saat transaksi pertama agar nomor barang mengikuti nota.
    with db.begin():
        begin_immediate(db)
        if repo.get_customer(db, customer_id) is None:
            raise NotFoundError("Pelanggan tidak ditemukan.")
        unit = VehicleOrUnit(
            customer_id=customer_id,
            unit_type="RADIATOR_ONLY",
            nopol=None,
            jenis_mobil=None,
            tahun_mobil=None,
            label_radiator=None,
            tipe_radiator=clean_tipe,
            keterangan=clean_ket,
        )
        db.add(unit)
        db.flush()
    db.refresh(unit)
    return unit


def describe_unit(unit: VehicleOrUnit) -> str:
    """Deskripsi unit untuk struk / riwayat."""
    if unit.unit_type == "MOBIL":
        text = unit.nopol or "-"
        if unit.jenis_mobil:
            text += f" ({unit.jenis_mobil})"
        return text
    text = "[BAWA RADIATOR SAJA]"
    if unit.label_radiator:
        text += f" {unit.label_radiator}"
    if unit.tipe_radiator:
        text += f" ({unit.tipe_radiator})"
    return text

FILE: services/receipt_service.py  (435 baris)
