"""Penyusun data struk & ringkasan transaksi servis."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from config import settings
from database.models import ServiceRecord
from services import settings_service, warranty_service, whatsapp_service
from services.customer_service import describe_unit
from services.escpos_service import ReceiptBuilder
from services.utils import format_rupiah, money, normalize_whatsapp


def _net_qty(item) -> int:
    return max(int(item.jumlah) - int(getattr(item, "jumlah_retur", 0) or 0), 0)


def _is_void(record: ServiceRecord) -> bool:
    return (getattr(record, "status", "NORMAL") or "NORMAL") == "VOID"


def _total_neto(record: ServiceRecord):
    if _is_void(record):
        return money(Decimal("0.00"))
    return money(record.total_servis - (record.total_retur or Decimal("0.00")))


def _sisa_bayar(record: ServiceRecord):
    if _is_void(record):
        return money(Decimal("0.00"))
    netto = money(
        max(
            Decimal(record.total_servis or 0)
            - Decimal(record.total_retur or 0),
            Decimal("0.00"),
        )
    )
    return money(max(netto - Decimal(record.total_dibayar or 0), Decimal("0.00")))


def _payment_label(payment) -> str:
    if payment.jenis in {"DP", "UANG_MUKA"}:
        return "Uang muka"
    if payment.jenis == "REFUND":
        return "Refund"
    return "Pembayaran"


def _duration_label(record: ServiceRecord) -> str:
    if not record.garansi_sampai:
        return ""
    days = (record.garansi_sampai - record.waktu_service.date()).days
    months = max(1, round(days / 30))
    return f" ({months} Bulan)"


def _label_item_name(unit) -> str:
    base = (unit.tipe_radiator or unit.jenis_mobil or "").strip()
    if not base:
        return "Radiator"
    if "radiator" in base.lower():
        return base
    return f"Radiator {base}"


def _label_unit_text(unit) -> str:
    if unit.unit_type == "MOBIL":
        text = unit.nopol or "-"
        if unit.jenis_mobil:
            text += f" ({unit.jenis_mobil})"
        return text
    return "[BAWA RADIATOR SAJA]"


def _labels(record: ServiceRecord) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Satu strip label untuk setiap barang berlabel pada nota.

    Skema transaksi saat ini hanya memiliki satu unit per nota; daftar tetap
    dipakai agar beberapa strip dapat dicetak bila unit bertambah.
    """
    unit = record.unit
    # Mobil utuh tidak memakai strip label, walau data label lama masih ada.
    if not unit or unit.unit_type != "RADIATOR_ONLY" or not unit.label_radiator:
        return []
    customer = unit.customer
    return [
        {
            "kode": unit.label_radiator,
            "nama": _label_item_name(unit),
            "unit": _label_unit_text(unit),
            "customer_nama": customer.nama,
            "customer_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
        }
    ]


def _append_label_strips(b: ReceiptBuilder, labels: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    cut = "- " * (b.width // 2)
    b.add(cut)
    b.center("POTONG DI SINI", bold=True)
    for index, label in enumerate(labels):
        if index:
            b.add(cut)
        b.field("Pelanggan", label["customer_nama"])
        b.field("No. HP", label["customer_hp"])
        b.field("Unit", label["unit"])
        b.add(label["kode"], bold=True)
        b.add(f"({label['nama']})")
    b.divider("=")


def _build(
    record: ServiceRecord,
    width: int,
    bengkel: Dict[str, str],
    payment=None,
    include_labels: bool = True,
) -> ReceiptBuilder:
    unit = record.unit
    customer = unit.customer

    b = ReceiptBuilder(width)
    b.divider("=")
    b.center(bengkel["nama"], bold=True, double=True)
    if bengkel["tagline"]:
        b.center(bengkel["tagline"])
    if bengkel["alamat"]:
        b.center(bengkel["alamat"])
    contact = bengkel["wa"] or bengkel["telepon"]
    if contact and contact != "-":
        b.center(f"Telp/WA: {contact}")
    b.divider("=")

    b.kv("No. Nota", record.no_nota)
    b.kv("Tanggal", record.waktu_service.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M"))
    b.kv("Kasir", "Kasir")
    if payment is not None:
        b.center("*** STRUK PELUNASAN ***", bold=True)
    if _is_void(record):
        b.divider("-")
        b.center("*** NOTA DIBATALKAN (VOID) ***", bold=True)
        if record.void_reason:
            b.field("Alasan", record.void_reason)
        if record.voided_at:
            b.field("Waktu void", record.voided_at.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M"))
        if record.voided_by:
            b.field("Oleh", record.voided_by)
    b.divider("-")

    b.field("Pelanggan", customer.nama)
    b.field("No. HP", customer.nomor_hp)
    b.field("Unit", describe_unit(unit))
    b.field("Keluhan", record.keluhan_awal or "-")
    if record.catatan_pengerjaan:
        b.field("Catatan", record.catatan_pengerjaan)
    b.divider("-")

    if record.jobs:
        b.add("JASA:", bold=True)
        for job in record.jobs:
            b.item(job.deskripsi, job.jumlah, job.harga, job.subtotal)
    if record.parts:
        b.add("PART:", bold=True)
        for part in record.parts:
            b.item(part.nama_part, part.jumlah, part.harga_satuan, part.subtotal)
    if not record.jobs and not record.parts:
        b.add("(tidak ada rincian pengerjaan)")

    b.divider("-")
    b.kv("Total Jasa", format_rupiah(record.biaya_jasa))
    b.kv("Total Part", format_rupiah(record.total_part))
    if record.diskon and record.diskon > 0:
        b.kv("Diskon", "-" + format_rupiah(record.diskon))
    b.kv("Grand Total", format_rupiah(record.total_servis), bold=True, double=True)
    total_retur = record.total_retur or Decimal("0.00")
    if total_retur > 0 or _is_void(record):
        b.kv("Retur", "-" + format_rupiah(total_retur))
        b.kv("Total Neto", format_rupiah(_total_neto(record)), bold=True)

    if record.metode_bayar == "TRANSFER":
        b.kv("Metode Bayar", "TRANSFER BANK")
    else:
        b.kv("Metode Bayar", "CASH / TUNAI")
    b.kv("Bayar", format_rupiah(record.bayar))
    b.kv("Kembali", format_rupiah(record.kembalian))

    sisa = _sisa_bayar(record)
    if sisa > 0:
        b.divider("-")
        b.kv("Status Bayar", "BELUM LUNAS", bold=True)
        b.kv("Uang Muka / Dibayar", format_rupiah(record.total_dibayar or 0))
        b.kv("Sisa", format_rupiah(sisa), bold=True)
        if record.jatuh_tempo:
            b.kv("Jatuh Tempo", record.jatuh_tempo.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"))
    else:
        b.kv("Status Bayar", "LUNAS")

    payments = list(getattr(record, "payments", []) or [])
    if payment is not None or len(payments) > 1:
        b.divider("-")
        b.add("RIWAYAT PEMBAYARAN:", bold=True)
        for row in payments:
            label = _payment_label(row)
            if row.jenis == "PELUNASAN":
                label = f"Pelunasan {row.metode}"
            b.kv(label, format_rupiah(row.nominal))
        b.kv("Total Dibayar", format_rupiah(record.total_dibayar or 0))
        b.kv("Sisa", format_rupiah(sisa))
        if payment is not None:
            b.kv("Waktu Bayar", payment.waktu.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M"))
            b.kv("Metode", "TRANSFER BANK" if payment.metode == "TRANSFER" else "CASH / TUNAI")

    b.divider("-")
    b.add("GARANSI SERVIS:", bold=True)
    if _is_void(record):
        b.kv("Status", "DIBATALKAN")
    elif record.bergaransi and record.garansi_sampai:
        b.kv("Status", "BERGARANSI")
        b.kv(
            "Sampai",
            record.garansi_sampai.strftime("%d/%m/%Y") + _duration_label(record),
        )
        if record.catatan_garansi:
            b.field("Catatan", record.catatan_garansi)
    else:
        b.kv("Status", "TANPA GARANSI")

    b.divider("-")
    b.add("REKENING BENGKEL (TRANSFER):", bold=True)
    b.add(f"{bengkel['bank_nama']}: {bengkel['no_rekening']}")
    b.add(f"a/n {bengkel['atas_nama']}")

    b.divider("-")
    b.center("Simpan struk ini sebagai")
    b.center("kartu bukti garansi!")
    b.center("")
    b.center("Terima kasih!")
    b.divider("=")
    if include_labels:
        labels = _labels(record)
        if labels:
            _append_label_strips(b, labels)
    return b


def receipt_wa_text(record: ServiceRecord, bengkel: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """Teks nota lengkap untuk WhatsApp, tanpa strip label potong."""
    builder = _build(record, 32, bengkel, include_labels=False)
    return "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in builder.lines()).strip()


def build_receipt_payload(
    record: ServiceRecord,
    bengkel: Dict[str, str] | None = None,
    payment=None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Data lengkap struk (58mm & 80mm) untuk reprint / cetak Bluetooth."""
    unit = record.unit
    customer = unit.customer
    info = bengkel or settings_service.default_bengkel_info()

    builder_58 = _build(record, 32, info, payment)
    builder_80 = _build(record, 48, info, payment)
    labels = _labels(record)
    sisa = _sisa_bayar(record)
    payments = [
        {
            "id": row.id,
            "service_record_id": row.service_record_id,
            "jenis": row.jenis,
            "metode": row.metode,
            "nominal": row.nominal,
            "waktu": row.waktu,
            "operator": row.operator,
            "catatan": row.catatan,
            "request_id": row.request_id,
        }
        for row in (getattr(record, "payments", []) or [])
    ]

    jobs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
        {
            "id": job.id,
            "deskripsi": job.deskripsi,
            "jumlah": job.jumlah,
            "jumlah_retur": int(job.jumlah_retur or 0),
            "harga": job.harga,
            "subtotal": job.subtotal,
            "interval_servis_bulan": int(job.interval_servis_bulan or 0),
        }
        for job in record.jobs
    ]
    parts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
        {
            "id": part.id,
            "nama_part": part.nama_part,
            "jumlah": part.jumlah,
            "jumlah_retur": int(part.jumlah_retur or 0),
            "harga_satuan": part.harga_satuan,
            "subtotal": part.subtotal,
            "interval_servis_bulan": int(part.interval_servis_bulan or 0),
        }
        for part in record.parts
    ]

    return {
        "id": record.id,
        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
        "waktu_service": record.waktu_service,
        "customer_nama": customer.nama,
        "customer_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
        "unit_deskripsi": describe_unit(unit),
        "keluhan_awal": record.keluhan_awal,
        "catatan_pengerjaan": record.catatan_pengerjaan,
        "biaya_jasa": record.biaya_jasa,
        "total_part": record.total_part,
        "diskon": record.diskon,
        "total_servis": record.total_servis,
        "metode_bayar": record.metode_bayar,
        "bayar": record.bayar,
        "kembalian": record.kembalian,
        "status_bayar": "LUNAS" if _is_void(record) else (record.status_bayar or "LUNAS"),
        "jenis_bayar": record.jenis_bayar
        or ("KREDIT" if record.status_bayar == "BELUM_LUNAS" else "LUNAS"),
        "jatuh_tempo": record.jatuh_tempo,
        "rencana_bayar": money(record.rencana_bayar or 0),
        "total_dibayar": money(record.total_dibayar or 0),
        "sisa_bayar": sisa,
        "bergaransi": record.bergaransi,
        "garansi_sampai": record.garansi_sampai,
        "catatan_garansi": record.catatan_garansi,
        "status_klaim": record.status_klaim,
        "tanggal_klaim": record.tanggal_klaim,
        "garansi_aktif": warranty_service.is_active(record),
        "warranty_status": warranty_service.warranty_status(record),
        "status": record.status or "NORMAL",
        "void_reason": record.void_reason,
        "voided_at": record.voided_at,
        "voided_by": record.voided_by,
        "total_retur": record.total_retur or Decimal("0.00"),
        "retur_count": int(record.retur_count or 0),
        "total_neto": _total_neto(record),
        "labels": labels,
        "jobs": jobs,
        "parts": parts,
        "payments": payments,
        "wa_text": receipt_wa_text(record, info),
        "completion_wa_text": whatsapp_service.completion_wa_text(record, info),
        "customer_wa": normalize_whatsapp(customer.nomor_hp),
        "bengkel_nama": info["nama"],
        "bengkel_tagline": info["tagline"],
        "bengkel_alamat": info["alamat"],
        "bengkel_telepon": info["telepon"],
        "bengkel_wa": info["wa"],
        "bengkel_bank_nama": info["bank_nama"],
        "bengkel_no_rekening": info["no_rekening"],
        "bengkel_atas_nama": info["atas_nama"],
        "printer_paper_width": settings.printer_paper_width,
        "struk_58": builder_58.lines(),
        "struk_80": builder_80.lines(),
        "escpos_58_base64": builder_58.to_base64(),
        "escpos_80_base64": builder_80.to_base64(),
    }


def record_summary(record: ServiceRecord) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Ringkasan nota untuk tabel riwayat & dasbor."""
    unit = record.unit
    customer = unit.customer
    status = record.status or "NORMAL"
    laba_kotor = Decimal("0.00")
    modal_tidak_diketahui = False
    modal_estimasi = False
    if status != "VOID":
        for part in record.parts:
            net_qty = _net_qty(part)
            if net_qty <= 0:
                continue
            if part.harga_modal_estimasi:
                modal_estimasi = True
            modal = part.harga_modal
            if modal is None and part.stock_item is not None:
                modal = part.stock_item.harga_modal
            if modal is None:
                modal_tidak_diketahui = True
                continue
            laba_kotor += (part.harga_satuan - modal) * net_qty
    return {
        "id": record.id,
        "no_nota": record.no_nota,
        "waktu_service": record.waktu_service,
        "unit_id": unit.id,
        "customer_id": customer.id,
        "customer_nama": customer.nama,
        "customer_hp": customer.nomor_hp,
        "unit_deskripsi": describe_unit(unit),
        "keluhan_awal": record.keluhan_awal,
        "biaya_jasa": record.biaya_jasa,
        "total_part": record.total_part,
        "diskon": record.diskon,
        "total_servis": record.total_servis,
        "metode_bayar": record.metode_bayar,
        "bayar": record.bayar,
        "kembalian": record.kembalian,
        "status_bayar": "LUNAS" if status == "VOID" else (record.status_bayar or "LUNAS"),
        "jenis_bayar": record.jenis_bayar
        or ("KREDIT" if record.status_bayar == "BELUM_LUNAS" else "LUNAS"),
        "jatuh_tempo": record.jatuh_tempo,
        "rencana_bayar": money(record.rencana_bayar or 0),
        "total_dibayar": money(record.total_dibayar or 0),
        "sisa_bayar": _sisa_bayar(record),
        "bergaransi": record.bergaransi,
        "garansi_sampai": record.garansi_sampai,
        "catatan_garansi": record.catatan_garansi,
        "status_klaim": record.status_klaim,
        "tanggal_klaim": record.tanggal_klaim,
        "garansi_aktif": warranty_service.is_active(record),
        "warranty_status": warranty_service.warranty_status(record),
        "jumlah_item": len(record.jobs) + len(record.parts),
        "laba_kotor": laba_kotor,
        "modal_tidak_diketahui": modal_tidak_diketahui,
        "modal_estimasi": modal_estimasi,
        "status": status,
        "void_reason": record.void_reason,
        "voided_at": record.voided_at,
        "voided_by": record.voided_by,
        "total_retur": record.total_retur or Decimal("0.00"),
        "retur_count": int(record.retur_count or 0),
        "total_neto": _total_neto(record),
    }


__all__ = ["build_receipt_payload", "receipt_wa_text", "record_summary", "Decimal"]

FILE: services/utils.py  (108 baris)

"""Utilitas teks & angka bersama."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from typing import Any

TWOPLACES = Decimal("0.01")

_CONTROL_CHARS = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f]")
_SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")


def clean_text(value: Any, *, max_length: int | None = None, allow_newlines: bool = True) -> str:
    """Bersihkan karakter kontrol & rapikan spasi (bukan escape HTML)."""
    if value is None:
        return ""
    text = str(value).replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
    text = _CONTROL_CHARS.sub("", text)
    if not allow_newlines:
        text = text.replace("\n", " ").replace("\t", " ")
        text = _SPACES.sub(" ", text)
    if max_length is not None:
        text = text[:max_length]
    return text.strip()


def sanitize_ascii(value: Any) -> str:
    """Sanitasi teks untuk printer thermal: ASCII murni, emoji dibuang.

    Ref: PROMPT_AUDIT FASE 5.2.
    """
    text = clean_text(value, allow_newlines=False)
    normalized = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text)
    ascii_text = normalized.encode("ascii", "ignore").decode("ascii")
    return _SPACES.sub(" ", ascii_text).strip()


def to_decimal(value: Any) -> Decimal:
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return value
    if value is None or value == "":
        return Decimal("0")
    return Decimal(str(value))


def money(value: Any) -> Decimal:
    """Kuantisasi 2 desimal (half-up) untuk seluruh nominal uang."""
    return to_decimal(value).quantize(TWOPLACES, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


def format_rupiah(value: Any) -> str:
    amount = money(value)
    negative = amount < 0
    absolute = abs(amount)
    text = f"{absolute:,.2f}"
    if text.endswith(".00"):
        text = text[:-3]
    text = text.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")
    return ("-Rp " if negative else "Rp ") + text


def format_short_money(value: Any) -> str:
    """Nominal tanpa prefix Rp untuk kolom struk thermal yang sempit."""
    return format_rupiah(value).replace("Rp ", "")


def normalize_phone(raw: Any) -> str:
    """Normalisasi nomor HP: 0812-3456-7890 / +62 812... -> 081234567890."""
    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(raw or ""))
    if digits.startswith("62"):
        digits = "0" + digits[2:]
    elif digits.startswith("8"):
        digits = "0" + digits
    return digits


def normalize_whatsapp(raw: Any) -> str:
    """Normalisasi nomor WhatsApp: 08… / 8… / +62… menjadi 628…."""
    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(raw or ""))
    if digits.startswith("0"):
        digits = "62" + digits[1:]
    elif digits.startswith("8"):
        digits = "62" + digits
    elif digits.startswith("620"):
        digits = "62" + digits[3:]
    return digits


def is_valid_whatsapp(raw: Any) -> bool:
    digits = normalize_whatsapp(raw)
    return digits.startswith("62") and 10 <= len(digits) <= 15


def mask_phone(raw: Any) -> str:
    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(raw or ""))
    if len(digits) <= 7:
        return "****"
    return digits[:4] + "****" + digits[-3:]


def normalize_nopol(raw: Any) -> str:
    """Normalisasi nopol: 'b 1234 abc' -> 'B 1234 ABC'."""
    text = str(raw or "").upper()
    text = re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9 ]", " ", text)
    return _SPACES.sub(" ", text).strip()

"""Endpoint follow-up, servis berkala, garansi, dan piutang (Tahap 13)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from api.deps import require_auth
from api.schemas import (
    FollowupActionResultOut,
    FollowupCountsOut,
    FollowupDetailOut,
    FollowupItemOut,
    FollowupListOut,
    FollowupStatusIn,
    PengerjaanItemOut,
    PengerjaanListOut,
    WhatsAppAttemptIn,
    WhatsAppLogOut,
)
from database.session import get_db
from services import followup_service, pengerjaan_service
from services.auth_service import Operator

router = APIRouter(tags=["Follow-up"], dependencies=[Depends(require_auth)])


def _item_out(db: Session, item) -> FollowupItemOut:
    return FollowupItemOut.model_validate(
        followup_service.serialize_item(db, item)
    )


@router.get("/followup/items", response_model=FollowupListOut)
def list_followup_items(
    filter: str = Query(default="semua", max_length=32),
    jenis: Optional[str] = Query(default=None, max_length=24),
    customer_id: Optional[int] = Query(default=None, gt=0),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(default=None, max_length=24),
    exclude_status: Optional[str] = Query(default=None, max_length=24),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    return FollowupListOut.model_validate(
        followup_service.list_items(
            db,
            filter=filter,
            jenis=jenis,
            customer_id=customer_id,
            status=status,
            exclude_status=exclude_status,
        )
    )


@router.get("/followup/counts", response_model=FollowupCountsOut)
def followup_counts(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return FollowupCountsOut(**followup_service.counts(db))


@router.get("/pengerjaan", response_model=PengerjaanListOut)
def list_pengerjaan(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return PengerjaanListOut.model_validate(pengerjaan_service.list_items(db))


@router.post("/pengerjaan/{marker_id}/diambil", response_model=PengerjaanItemOut)
def mark_pengerjaan_taken(
    marker_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    operator: Operator = Depends(require_auth),
):
    marker = pengerjaan_service.mark_taken(db, marker_id, operator)
    return PengerjaanItemOut.model_validate(pengerjaan_service.serialize_item(marker))


@router.get("/followup/items/{item_id}", response_model=FollowupDetailOut)
def followup_detail(item_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return FollowupDetailOut.model_validate(followup_service.detail(db, item_id))


@router.post(
    "/followup/items/{item_id}/followup",
    response_model=FollowupActionResultOut,
)
def record_followup(
    item_id: int,
    payload: WhatsAppAttemptIn,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    operator: Operator = Depends(require_auth),
):
    result = followup_service.follow_up(
        db,
        item_id,
        payload.status,
        operator,
        catatan=payload.catatan,
        pesan=payload.pesan,
    )
    return FollowupActionResultOut(
        item=_item_out(db, result["item"]),
        wa_text=result["wa_text"],
        link=result["link"],
        log=WhatsAppLogOut.model_validate(result["log"]),
    )


@router.post(
    "/followup/items/{item_id}/status",
    response_model=FollowupItemOut,
)
def change_followup_status(
    item_id: int,
    payload: FollowupStatusIn,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    operator: Operator = Depends(require_auth),
):
    item = followup_service.change_status(
        db,
        item_id,
        payload.status,
        operator,
        alasan=payload.alasan,
        janji_bayar=payload.janji_bayar,
        tanggal_service=payload.tanggal_service,
    )
    return _item_out(db, item)

"""Endpoint pesan WhatsApp dan log upaya (Tahap 12)."""

from typing import List

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

@router.post(
    "/service-records/{record_id}/whatsapp",
    response_model=WhatsAppAttemptOut,
    status_code=201,
)
def record_whatsapp_attempt(
    record_id: int,
    payload: WhatsAppAttemptIn,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    operator: Operator = Depends(require_auth),
):
    log, wa_text, link = whatsapp_service.log_attempt(
        db,
        record_id,
        payload.status,
        operator,
        catatan=payload.catatan,
        jenis=payload.jenis,
    )
    return WhatsAppAttemptOut(
        status=log.status,
        nomor_masked=log.nomor_masked,
        link=link,
        wa_text=wa_text,
        log=WhatsAppLogOut.model_validate(log),
    )

        WhatsAppLogOut.model_validate(log)

"""Endpoint pengaturan identitas bengkel (khusus admin)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from api.deps import require_owner
from api.schemas import (
    BengkelInfoIn,
    BengkelInfoOut,
    OpsSettingsIn,
    OpsSettingsOut,
    ScheduledBackupOut,
    TelegramInfoOut,
    TelegramSettingsIn,
)
from database.session import get_db
from services import settings_service, telegram_backup_service

router = APIRouter(tags=["Pengaturan"], dependencies=[Depends(require_owner)])


@router.get(
    "/settings/ops",
    response_model=OpsSettingsOut,
)
def get_ops(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return OpsSettingsOut(**settings_service.get_ops_settings(db))


@router.put(
    "/settings/ops",
    response_model=OpsSettingsOut,
)
def update_ops(payload: OpsSettingsIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    return OpsSettingsOut(
        **settings_service.update_ops_settings(
            db, payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
        )
    )


@router.put(
    "/settings/bengkel",
    response_model=BengkelInfoOut,
)
def update_bengkel(payload: BengkelInfoIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    info = settings_service.update_bengkel_info(db, payload.model_dump())
    return BengkelInfoOut(**info)


@router.put(
    "/settings/telegram",
    response_model=TelegramInfoOut,
)
def update_telegram(payload: TelegramSettingsIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    config = settings_service.update_telegram_config(db, payload.model_dump())
    return TelegramInfoOut(**settings_service.telegram_public_info(config))


@router.post(
    "/admin/scheduled-backup",
    response_model=ScheduledBackupOut,
)
def scheduled_backup(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Dipicu WorkManager Android; aman diulang tanpa membuat file ganda."""
    return ScheduledBackupOut(
        **telegram_backup_service.run_scheduled_backup_now(db)
    )


@router.post(
    "/admin/backup-now",
)
def backup_now(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Snapshot terkini + kirim Telegram bila dikonfigurasi; lokal selalu dibuat."""
    return telegram_backup_service.run_manual_backup(db)


@router.post(
    "/admin/telegram-backup",
)
def send_telegram_backup(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    result = telegram_backup_service.send_backup(db)
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "filename": result["filename"],
        "start": result["start"],
        "end": result["end"],
        "sent_at": result["sent_at"],
        "jumlah_nota": result["jumlah_nota"],
    }

"""Skema Pydantic (kontrak REST API).

Ref: PRD BAB 5.1.

Kompatibel dengan Pydantic v1 maupun v2:
- Server desktop memakai v2 (requirements.txt).
- APK Android memakai v1 karena pydantic-core (Rust) tidak tersedia di Chaquopy.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pydantic
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

PYDANTIC_V2 = pydantic.VERSION.startswith("2.")

if PYDANTIC_V2:
    from typing import Annotated

    from pydantic import ConfigDict, PlainSerializer

    def _format_money(value: Any) -> str:
        return f"{Decimal(value):.2f}"

    Money = Annotated[
        Decimal,
        PlainSerializer(_format_money, return_type=str, when_used="json"),
    ]
    ZERO_MONEY: Any = Decimal("0.00")

    class CompatBase(BaseModel):
        pass

    class StrictBase(CompatBase):
        """Menolak field tak dikenal, mis. identitas operator dari frontend."""

        model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    class ORMModel(CompatBase):
        model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    def _pattern(regex: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {"pattern": regex}

else:  # Pydantic v1

    class Money(str):
        """Decimal uang yang diserialisasi sebagai string dua desimal."""

        @classmethod
        def __get_validators__(cls):
            yield cls.validate

        @classmethod
        def validate(cls, value: Any) -> "Money":
            if isinstance(value, Money):
                return value
            return cls(f"{Decimal(value):.2f}")

        @classmethod
        def __modify_schema__(cls, field_schema: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            field_schema.update(type="string", example="0.00")

    ZERO_MONEY = Money("0.00")

    class CompatBase(BaseModel):
        class Config:
            orm_mode = True

        @classmethod
        def model_validate(cls, obj: Any, **_kwargs: Any):
            if isinstance(obj, dict):
                return cls.parse_obj(obj)
            return cls.from_orm(obj)

        def model_dump(self, **kwargs: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
            return self.dict(**kwargs)

    class StrictBase(CompatBase):
        class Config(CompatBase.Config):
            extra = "forbid"

    class ORMModel(CompatBase):
        pass

    def _pattern(regex: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {"regex": regex}


# --- CUSTOMER & UNIT ---
class CustomerCreate(CompatBase):
    nama: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=255)
    nomor_hp: str = Field(..., max_length=64)
    alamat: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=500)


class CustomerOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    nama: str
    nomor_hp: str
    alamat: Optional[str] = None
    created_at: datetime


class UnitCreate(CompatBase):
    customer_id: int = Field(..., gt=0)
    unit_type: str = Field(..., **_pattern("^(MOBIL|RADIATOR_ONLY)$"))
    nopol: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=32)
    jenis_mobil: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=255)
    tahun_mobil: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1900, le=2100)
    tipe_radiator: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=255)
    keterangan: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=1000)


class UnitOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    customer_id: int
    unit_type: str
    nopol: Optional[str] = None
    jenis_mobil: Optional[str] = None
    tahun_mobil: Optional[int] = None
    label_radiator: Optional[str] = None
    tipe_radiator: Optional[str] = None
    keterangan: Optional[str] = None
    deskripsi: Optional[str] = None
    created_at: datetime

    total_radiator: int
    units: List[UnitOut]

    garansi_akan_habis: int = 0

    followup_aktif: int = 0
    prospek_count: int = 0
    berkendala_prospek: int = 0
    akan_datang_servis: int = 0
    akan_datang_garansi: int = 0
    akan_datang_prospek: int = 0
    kendaraan_selesai: int = 0

FILE: frontend/js/app.js - helpers, waktu, dan format
==============================================================================

  /* ----------------------------- Helpers --------------------------------- */
  const $ = (selector, root) => (root || document).querySelector(selector);
  const $$ = (selector, root) =>
    Array.from((root || document).querySelectorAll(selector));

  const HTML_ESCAPES = {
    '&': '&amp;',
    '<': '&lt;',
    '>': '&gt;',
    '"': '&quot;',
    "'": '&#39;',
  };

  function esc(value) {
    return String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(
      /[&<>"']/g,
      (char) => HTML_ESCAPES[char]
    );
  }

  // Uang kasir selalu rupiah bulat. Pemisah ribuan (titik/koma/spasi) boleh,
  // tetapi format desimal ditolak agar angka yang dikirim tidak ambigu.
  const MONEY_INPUT_RE = /^\d{1,3}(?:[.,\s]\d{3})*$|^\d+$/;

  function moneyInputValid(value) {
    const raw = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    return raw === '' || MONEY_INPUT_RE.test(raw);
  }

  function parseMoney(value) {
    const raw = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    if (!raw || !MONEY_INPUT_RE.test(raw)) return 0;
    const digits = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
    return digits ? Number(digits) : 0;
  }

  function formatMoneyValue(value) {
    const digits = String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/\D/g, '')
      .replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
    if (!digits) return '';
    return digits.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.');
  }

  function setMoneyValue(input, value) {
    if (!input) return;
    input.value = value == null || value === '' ? '' : formatMoneyValue(value);
  }

  function formatMoneyField(input) {
    if (!input) return;
    const raw = input.value;
    const caret =
      typeof input.selectionStart === 'number'
        ? input.selectionStart
        : raw.length;
    const digitsBefore = raw.slice(0, caret).replace(/\D/g, '').length;
    const formatted = formatMoneyValue(raw);
    if (input.value === formatted) return;
    input.value = formatted;
    if (document.activeElement !== input) return;
    let position = 0;
    let seen = 0;
    while (position < formatted.length && seen < digitsBefore) {
      if (/\d/.test(formatted[position])) seen += 1;
      position += 1;
    }
    try {
      input.setSelectionRange(position, position);
    } catch (err) {
      /* Input tanpa dukungan seleksi caret tetap memakai nilai baru. */
    }
  }

  function isMoneyField(target) {
    if (!target) return false;
    if (target.classList.contains('money-input')) return true;
    return (
      target.matches('[data-kind][data-field]') &&
      /^(harga|harga_satuan|harga_modal)$/.test(target.dataset.field)
    );
  }

  function readMoneyInput(input, label) {
    const raw = input ? input.value : '';
    if (!moneyInputValid(raw)) {
      toast(
        label + ' harus berupa rupiah bulat tanpa desimal. Contoh: 150.000.',
        'warning'
      );
      if (input) input.focus();
      return null;
    }
    return parseMoney(raw);
  }

  // Nilai uang dari API berupa string desimal dua tempat; penjumlahan untuk
  // tampilan dilakukan dalam sen agar tidak ada galat floating point.
  function moneyToCents(value) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value).trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    const negative = text.charAt(0) === '-';
    const [whole, fraction] = text.replace('-', '').split('.');
    const cents =
      Number(whole || 0) * 100 + Number(((fraction || '') + '00').slice(0, 2));
    return negative ? -cents : cents;
  }

  function centsToRp(cents) {
    return fmtRp(Math.round(cents) / 100);
  }

  function intValue(value, fallback) {
    const parsed = parseInt(String(value == null ? '' : value), 10);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : fallback;
  }

  function fmtRp(value) {
    const number = Number(value);
    const safe = Number.isFinite(number) ? Math.round(number) : 0;
    return 'Rp ' + safe.toLocaleString('id-ID');
  }

  function toISODate(date) {
    const offset = date.getTimezoneOffset();
    return new Date(date.getTime() - offset * 60000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }

  function todayISO() {
    return toISODate(new Date());
  }

  function followupWindow() {
    const ops = (state.config && state.config.ops) || {};
    const start = ops.followup_jam_mulai || '08:00';
    const end = ops.followup_jam_selesai || '20:00';
    return {
      start,
      end,
      label: start.replace(':', '.') + '-' + end.replace(':', '.'),
    };
  }

  function isFollowupWindowOpen(now) {
    const windowInfo = followupWindow();
    const toMinutes = (value) => {
      const parts = String(value || '').split(':');
      return intValue(parts[0], 0) * 60 + intValue(parts[1], 0);
    };
    const start = toMinutes(windowInfo.start);
    const end = toMinutes(windowInfo.end);
    if (start === end) return true;
    const current = now || new Date();
    const minutes = current.getHours() * 60 + current.getMinutes();
    return start < end
      ? minutes >= start && minutes < end
      : minutes >= start || minutes < end;
  }

  function fmtDate(dateString) {
    if (!dateString) return '-';
    const value = String(dateString).slice(0, 10);
    const date = new Date(value + 'T00:00:00');
    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;
    return date.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  }

  function fmtDateTime(value) {
    if (!value) return '-';
    const date = new Date(value);
    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return String(value);
    return date.toLocaleString('id-ID', {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
  }

  function debounce(fn, delay) {
    let timer = null;
    return function debounced() {
      const args = arguments;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(null, args), delay);
    };
  }

  function describeUnit(unit) {
    if (!unit) return '-';
    if (unit.unit_type === 'MOBIL') {
      let text = unit.nopol || '-';
      if (unit.jenis_mobil) text += ' (' + unit.jenis_mobil + ')';
      return text;
    }
    let text = 'Radiator saja · ' + (unit.label_radiator || 'belum berlabel');
    if (unit.tipe_radiator) text += ' (' + unit.tipe_radiator + ')';
    return text;
  }


==============================================================================
FILE: frontend/js/app.js - navigasi
==============================================================================

  /* ----------------------------- Navigation ------------------------------- */
  function navigate(view) {
    $$('.nav-tab').forEach((tab) => {
      tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.dataset.view === view);
    });
    $$('.view').forEach((section) => section.classList.add('hidden'));
    const target = $('#view-' + view);
    if (target) target.classList.remove('hidden');
    const activeTab = $('.nav-tab.active');
    if (activeTab && activeTab.scrollIntoView) {
      activeTab.scrollIntoView({
        block: 'nearest',
        inline: 'center',
        behavior: 'smooth',
      });
    }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });

    if (view === 'dashboard') loadDashboard();
    if (view === 'followup') loadFollowupPage();
    if (view === 'history') loadHistory($('#historySearch').value.trim());
    if (view === 'stock') renderStock();
    if (view === 'jasa') renderJasa();
    if (view === 'customers') {
      loadCustomerDirectory($('#customerDirectorySearch').value.trim());
    }
    if (view === 'report') loadReport();
    if (view === 'settings') {
      fillSettingsForm();
      fillOpsForm();
      fillTelegramForm();
      initPrinterSettings();
    }
    if (view === 'pos' && !state.selectedCustomer) {
      const search = $('#customerSearch');
      if (search) search.focus();
    }
  }


==============================================================================
FILE: frontend/js/app.js - waLink dan pratinjau WhatsApp
==============================================================================

  function waLink(number, text) {
    let target = waDigits(number);
    if (!target) return '';
    if (target.charAt(0) === '0') {
      target = '62' + target.slice(1);
    } else if (target.charAt(0) === '8') {
      target = '62' + target;
    }
    const suffix = text ? '?text=' + encodeURIComponent(text) : '';
    return 'https://wa.me/' + target + suffix;
  }

  async function openExternalLink(url) {
    const target = String(url || '').trim();
    if (!target) return false;
    if (
      hasNativeBridge() &&
      window.BengkelBridge &&
      typeof window.BengkelBridge.call === 'function'
    ) {
      try {
        await window.BengkelBridge.call('openExternal', { url: target }, 15000);
        return true;
      } catch (err) {
        toast(err.message || 'Tautan tidak dapat dibuka.', 'error', 6000);
        return false;
      }
    }
    const opened = window.open(target, '_blank');
    if (!opened) {
      toast('Tautan tidak dapat dibuka. Izinkan pop-up lalu coba lagi.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }
    opened.opener = null;
    return true;
  }

  async function copyText(value, successMessage) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);
    if (!text.trim()) {
      toast('Tidak ada teks untuk disalin.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      } else {
        const area = document.createElement('textarea');
        area.value = text;
        area.style.position = 'fixed';
        area.style.opacity = '0';
        document.body.appendChild(area);
        area.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        area.remove();
      }
      toast(successMessage || 'Teks disalin.', 'success');
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      toast('Gagal menyalin. Salin manual dari kotak teks.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }
  }

  function openWaPreview(options) {
    const opts = options || {};
    const link = opts.link || waLink(opts.nomor, opts.text);
    state.waPreview = {
      link: link,
      nomor: opts.nomor || '',
      disabled: Boolean(opts.disabled),
      onOpen: typeof opts.onOpen === 'function' ? opts.onOpen : null,
    };
    $('#waModalTitle').textContent = opts.judul || 'Pesan WhatsApp';
    $('#waModalSubtitle').textContent =
      opts.subtitle || 'Periksa pesan sebelum dibuka di WhatsApp.';
    $('#waModalNumber').value = opts.masked || opts.nomor || '-';
    $('#waModalText').value = opts.text || '';
    $('#waModalNote').textContent =
      opts.note ||
      'Pesan hanya dibuka di WhatsApp, tidak dikirim otomatis. Upaya dicatat saat tombol dibuka.';
    const open = $('#waModalOpen');
    if (!link || opts.disabled) {
      open.classList.add('hidden');
    } else {
      open.classList.remove('hidden');
      open.href = link;
    }
    openModal('#waModal');
  }

  async function handleWaOpen(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const preview = state.waPreview;
    if (!preview || preview.disabled) return;
    const text = ($('#waModalText').value || '').trim();
    const link = preview.nomor ? waLink(preview.nomor, text) : preview.link;
    if (!link) {
      toast('Nomor WhatsApp pelanggan belum valid.', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    const onOpen = preview.onOpen;
    state.waPreview = null;
    closeModal('#waModal');
    const opening = openExternalLink(link);
    if (!onOpen) {
      await opening;
      return;
    }
    try {
      await onOpen(text);
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, 'error', 7000);
    }
    await opening;
  }

  function receiptWaNumber(receipt) {
    return receipt.customer_wa || receipt.customer_hp || '';
  }


==============================================================================
FILE: frontend/js/app.js - pesan kendaraan selesai
==============================================================================

  async function openCompletionWhatsapp(id) {
    try {
      let receipt = state.detailRecord;
      if (!receipt || receipt.id !== id) {
        receipt = await API.receipt(id);
      }
      if (!waDigits(receiptWaNumber(receipt))) {
        toast('Nomor WhatsApp pelanggan belum valid.', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      if (!(receipt.completion_wa_text || '').trim()) {
        toast('Pesan kendaraan selesai belum tersedia.', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      openWaPreview({
        judul: 'Kendaraan selesai · ' + receipt.no_nota,
        subtitle: 'Periksa pesan untuk ' + receipt.customer_nama + '.',
        nomor: receiptWaNumber(receipt),
        text: receipt.completion_wa_text,
        note: 'Membuka WhatsApp akan mencatat upaya pemberitahuan. Kasir tetap menekan kirim di WhatsApp.',
        onOpen: async () => {
          await API.recordWhatsapp(receipt.id, {
            status: 'DIBUKA',
            jenis: 'piutang',
          });
          toast('Pemberitahuan kendaraan selesai tercatat.', 'success');
          if (state.detailRecord && state.detailRecord.id === receipt.id) {
            await loadRecordExtras(receipt.id);
          }
          if (!$('#pengerjaanModal').classList.contains('hidden')) {
            await loadPengerjaan();
          }
        },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      toast('Pesan kendaraan selesai gagal dimuat.', 'error', 6000);
    }
  }


==============================================================================
FILE: frontend/js/app.js - seluruh fungsi follow-up
